   6880AND #&E0:STA &F1    :\ Save temporarily
   6900\ Manipulate stuff to set serial word size
   6910JSR L85A4           :\ Select control register &10
   6920LDA L84BE,X         :\
   6930AND #&1F            :\
   6940ORA &F1             :\
   6950STA &FC60           :\ Write to control register
   6960LDA &FC60           :\ Read from control register
   6970AND #&F0            :\ Keep b7-b4
   6980LDY L84BE,X
   6990BMI L8469           :\ If b7 set, skip
   7000ORA #&0F            :\ If b7 clear, set b3-b0
   7010.L8469
   7020ORA #&07            :\ Set to %xxxxx111
   7030STA &FC60           :\ Write to control register
   7040.L846E
   7050JMP L80D1           :\ Claim call and return
   7070\ Turn off serial input
   7080\ ---------------------
   7090.L8471
   7100LDA #&02:STA &FC62  :\ Disable receiver
   7110JSR L8598           :\ Reset reciever
   7120JSR L85A0           :\ Reset error status
   7130LDA &0D6A:AND #&FD  :\ Reset serial input IRQ
   7140STA &0D6A:STA &FC65 :\ Write to IRQ mask and serial port IRQ register
   7150JSR L82EB           :\ Set/Clear CTS
   7160LDA #&10:JSR L85FD  :\ Clear b4 of flags, turn off polling
   7170JMP L80D1           :\ Claim call and return
   7190.L8492
   7200LDA #&9F            :\ *FX156,x,159 for a serial break
   7210CMP &F1:BNE L846E   :\ Not Y=159, exit
   7230\ Y=159, start/stop a serial break
   7240\ --------------------------------
   7250BIT &F0:BNE L846E   :\ X must be %0xx00000
   7260LDA &F0             :\ Get X parameter
   7270CMP #&60:BEQ L84AA  :\ *FX156,96,159 - start a break
   7280LDA #&70:STA &FC62  :\ Else, stop transmitting a break
   7290JMP L846E           :\ Claim call and return
   7310.L84AA
   7320LDA #&64:STA &FC62  :\ Start transmitting a break
   7330JMP L846E           :\ Claim call and return
   7350\ Baud rate look-up table
   7360\ -----------------------
   7370.L84B2
   7380EQUB &BB:EQUB &00:EQUB &33  :\ 0=9600, 1=75,   2=150
   7390EQUB &44:EQUB &66:EQUB &88  :\ 3=300,  4=1200, 5=2400
   7400EQUB &99:EQUB &BB:EQUB &CC  :\ 6=4800, 7=9600, 8=19200
   7410EQUB &11:EQUB &55:EQUB &AA  :\ 9=110,  10=600, 11=1800
   7430\ Serial control look-up table
   7440\ ----------------------------
   7450.L84BE
   7460EQUB &02:EQUB &06:EQUB &82:EQUB &86
   7470EQUB &13:EQUB &93:EQUB &83:EQUB &87
   7490\ Initialise serial interface
   7500\ ---------------------------
   7510\ CC=use current settings, CS=use default settings
   7520.L8567
   7530LDA #0:STA &0D6A      :\ DMB, ignore serial interupts
   7540JSR L85A4             :\ Reset MC6281 MR pointer
   7550LDA #&93:STA &FC60    :\ First write, RTS=enabled, NoParity, 8bit
   7560LDA #&07:STA &FC60    :\ Second write, StopBit=1 bit length
   7570EOR &FC60:BNE L8597   :\ DMB, read back, if hardware absent, exit
   7580LDA #&8F:STA &FC64    :\ Set AuxControl to Baud Rate 2, IRQs on
   7590LDA &0D69:BCC L8580   :\ Get current baud setting
   7600LDA #&BB              :\ Default baud rate=9600
   7610.L8580
   7620STA &0D69:STA &FC61   :\ Set baud register and RAM copy
   7630JSR L8598             :\ Reset 6821 receiver
   7640JSR L859C             :\ Reset 6821 transmitter
   7650JSR L85A0             :\ Reset 6821 error status
   7660LDA #&80:STA &0D6A    :\ Set IRQ mask to allow serial interupts
   7670STA &FC65             :\ Enable 6821 interupts
   7680.L8597
   7690RTS
   7710.L8598
   7720LDA #&20:BNE L85A6  :\ &20=Reset Receiver
   7730.L859C
   7740LDA #&30:BNE L85A6  :\ &30=Reset Transmitter
   7750.L85A0
   7760LDA #&40:BNE L85A6  :\ &40=Reset Error Status
   7770.L85A4
   7780LDA #&10            :\ &10=Reset MR pointer
   7790.L85A6
   7800STA &FC62:RTS       :\ Write to command register
   7820.L85AA
   7830LDA &0D6A:ORA #&01  :\ Set serial buffer active
   7840STA &0D6A:STA &FC65 :\ Write to IRQ mask and serial port IRQ register
   7850LDA #&04:BNE L85A6  :\ Write &04=Transmitter Enabled to command register
   7870.L85B9
   7880LDA &0D6A:AND #&FE  :\ Set serial buffer inactive
   7890STA &0D6A:STA &FC65 :\ Write to IRQ mask and serial port IRQ register
   7900LDA #&08:BNE L85A6  :\ Write &08=Transmitter Disabled to command register
   7920.L85C8
   7930LDX #&01            :\ X=1 - serial input buffer
   7940JMP (&022A)         :\ Jump to INSV
   7960.L85CD
   7970CLV                 :\ CLV=remove character
   7980LDX #&02            :\ X=2 - serial output buffer
   7990JMP (&022C)         :\ Jump to REMV
   8010.L85D3
   8020CLV                 :\ CLV=remove character
   8030LDX #&03            :\ X=3 - printer buffer
   8040JMP (&022C)         :\ Jump to REMV
   8060\ Count space left in serial input buffer
   8070\ ---------------------------------------
   8080\ Returns CS=more than 8 bytes left
   8090\         CC=less than 8 bytes left
   8100.L85D9
   8110JSR L85E3           :\ Count space in serial input buffer
   8120CPY #&01:BCS L85E2  :\ If >&00FF, return CS
   8130CPX #&09            :\ If >&0008, return CS
   8140.L85E2
   8150RTS
   8170.L85E3
   8180SEC                 :\ SEC=count space left
   8190CLV                 :\ CLV=count buffer
   8200LDX #&01            :\ X=1 - serial input buffer
   8210JMP (&022E)         :\ Jump to CNPV
   8230.L85EA
   8240ORA &0D68           :\ Merge into current flags
   8250LDX &0D68           :\ Get old value of flags to X
   8260STA &0D68           :\ Store updated flags
   8270TXA                 :\ Get old flags into A
   8280AND #&F0:BNE L85E2  :\ If old flags had polling enabled, exit
   8290:                   :\ We've changed to needing polling, increment semaphore
   8300.L85F8
   8310LDA #&16:JMP OSBYTE :\ Inc. ROM polling semaphore
   8330.L85FD
   8340TAX                 :\ Save mask in X
   8350LDA #&F0:BIT &0D68  :\ Check current polling flags
   8360BEQ L85E2           :\ No flags have polling enabled, exit
   8370TXA:EOR #&FF        :\ Get mask back and toggle it
   8380AND &0D68:STA &0D68 :\ Remove the bit and update flags
   8390AND #&F0:BNE L85E2  :\ If any polling enabled, exit
   8400:                   :\ Nothing left needing polling, decrement semaphor
   8410.L8612
   8420LDA #&17:JMP OSBYTE :\ Dec. ROM polling semaphore
   8450\ SERVICE 6 - Error
   8460\ =================
   8470.L8617
   8480LDA #&08:BIT &0D68:BEQ L8622  :\ Not temporarily disabled, skip
   8490LDA L0D6C:STA &0249           :\ Restore ROM paging semaphore
   8500.L8622
   8510LDA #&01:BIT &0D68:BNE L863E  :\ Plus 1 disabled, ignore error
   8520LDA &EF:PHA
   8530LDA &F0:PHA:LDA &F1:PHA       :\ Save OSBYTE/OSWORD parameters
   8540JSR L8671
   8550PLA:STA &F1:PLA:STA &F0
   8560PLA:STA &EF
   8570.L863E
   8580JMP L80CC                     :\ Restore and return unclaimed
   8610\ Disable Plus 1 functions
   8620\ ========================
   8630.L8641
   8640PHA:TXA:PHA:TYA:PHA
   8650LDA &0D68:BMI L865C :\ Bit 7 set, already disabled
   8660PHA
   8670ORA #&80:STA &0D68  :\ Set bit 7 of flags
   8680PLA:BEQ L865C       :\ No handlers to disable
   8690JSR L8612           :\ Decrement ROM polling semaphore
   8700LDA #&01:STA &FC6F  :\ Raise 'RTS', stop serial sender
   8710.L865C
   8720PLA:TAY:PLA:TAX:PLA
   8730RTS
   8760\ Enable Plus 1 functions
   8770\ =======================
   8780\ Called after calling filing vector, so all four registers need to be saved
   8790.L8662
   8800PHP:PHA
   8810LDA L0D6C:STA &0249 :\ Restore ROM paging semaphore
   8820LDA &0D68:AND #&F7
   8830STA &0D68           :\ Clear 'disabled' flag
   8840PLA:PLP
   8850.L8671
   8860PHP:PHA:TXA:PHA:TYA:PHA
   8870LDA &0D68:BPL L8689 :\ Bit 7 clear, already enabled
   8880AND #&7F:STA &0D68  :\ Clear bit 7 of flags
   8890BEQ L8689           :\ No handlers to enable
   8900JSR L85F8           :\ Increment ROM polling semaphore
   8910JSR L82EB           :\ Restore CTS state
   8920.L8689
   8930PLA:TAY:PLA:TAX:PLA:PLP
   8940RTS
   8970\ SERVICE &0F - Vectors changed
   8980\ =============================
   8990.L8690
   9000LDX &0213:INX:BEQ L86A3   :\ FILEV not pointing to MOS, exit
   9010LDA #&00:TAY:JSR OSARGS   :\ Read current filing system
   9020CMP #&03:BCS L86A3        :\ Not TAPE/ROM, exit
   9030CMP #&00:BNE L86B4
   9040.L86A3
   9050JMP L80CC
   9070\ Filing system vectors
   9080\ ---------------------
   9090.L86A6
   9100EQUW L8736 :\ FILEV
   9110EQUW L875F :\ ARGSV
   9120EQUW L8709 :\ BGETV
   9130EQUW L8712 :\ BPUTV
   9140EQUW L871F :\ GBPBV
   9150EQUW L873F :\ FINDV
   9160EQUW L874C :\ FSCV
   9180\ Vectors changed, now using TAPE/ROM
   9190\ -----------------------------------
   9200.L86B4
   9210PHP:SEI                      :\ Disable IRQs while changing vectors
   9220LDY #&1B:LDX #&12
   9230.X86D1
   9240LDA &0200,X:STA L0D70-&12,X  :\ Copy filing system vectors
   9250TYA:STA &0200,X              :\ to L0D70-L0D7D and redirect them
   9260LDA &0201,X:STA L0D70-&11,X  :\ to extended vectors
   9270LDA #&FF:STA &0201,X
   9280LDA L86A6-&12,X:STA &0D9F,Y  :\ Claim extended filing system vectors
   9290INX:INY
   9300LDA L86A6-&12,X:STA &0D9F,Y
   9310INX:INY
   9320LDA &F4:STA &0D9F,Y
   9330INY:CPX #&20:BNE X86D1
   9340PLP
   9350.L8706
   9360JMP L80CC
   9380\ Redirected BGETV
   9390\ ----------------
   9400.L8709
   9410JSR L877E:JSR L879C:JMP L8662 :\ Disable Plus 1, call BGET, enable Plus 1
   9430\ Redirected BPUTV
   9440\ ----------------
   9450.L8712
   9460JSR L877E:JSR L879F:JMP L8662 :\ Disable Plus 1, call BPUT, enable Plus 1
   9480\ Pass OSGBPB 5-7, 9+ on without disabling Plus 1
   9490\ -----------------------------------------------
   9500.L871B
   9510PLP:JMP L87A2
   9530\ Redirected GBPBV
   9540\ ----------------
   9550.L871F
   9560PHP
   9570CMP #&08:BEQ L872C  :\ Pass OSGBPB 8 on via veneer
   9580CMP #&05:BCS L871B  :\ Pass OSGBPB 5-7, 9+ on directly
   9590CMP #&01:BCC L871B  :\ Pass OSGBPB 0 on directly
   9600.L872C
   9610PLP
   9620JSR L877E:JSR L87A2:JMP L8662 :\ Disable Plus 1, call GBPB, enable Plus 1
   9640\ Redirected FILEV
   9650\ ----------------
   9660.L8736
   9670JSR L877E:JSR L8796:JMP L8662 :\ Disable Plus 1, call FILE, enable Plus 1
   9690\ Redirected FINDV
   9700\ ----------------
   9710.L873F
   9720JSR L877E:JSR L87A5:JMP L8662 :\ Disable Plus 1, call FIND, enable Plus 1
   9740\ Pass FSCV on without disabling Plus 1
   9750\ -------------------------------------
   9760.L8748
   9770PLP:JMP L87A8
   9790\ Redirected FSCV
   9800\ ---------------
   9810.L874C
   9820PHP
   9830CMP #&02:BCC L8748  :\ Pass <2 on without disabling
   9840CMP #&06:BCS L8748  :\ Pass 6+ on without disabling
   9850PLP                 :\ Pass 2-5 via veneer
   9860JSR L877E:JSR L87A8:JMP L8662 :\ Disable Plus 1, call FSCV, enable Plus 1
   9880\ Redirected ARGSV
   9890\ ----------------
   9900.L875F
   9910PHP
   9920CMP #&FF:BEQ L8774  :\ Ensure buffers - pass via veneer
   9930CPY #&00:BEQ L8770  :\ Read filing info - pass on directly
   9940CMP #&01:BEQ L8774  :\ PTR= - pass on via veneer
   9950CMP #&03:BEQ L8774  :\ EXT= - pass on via veneer
   9960.L8770
   9970PLP:JMP L8799       :\ Pass on directly
   9990.L8774
  10000PLP
  10010JSR L877E:JSR L8799:JMP L8662 :\ Disable Plus 1, call ARGSV, enable Plus 1
  10040\ Temporarily disable Plus 1
  10050\ ==========================
  10060.L877E
  10070PHP:PHA
  10080JSR L8641           :\ Disable Plus 1
  10090LDA &0249:STA L0D6C :\ Save ROM semaphore
  10100LDA #&00:STA &0249  :\ Clear ROM semaphore
  10110LDA &0D68:ORA #&08
  10120STA &0D68           :\ Set 'disabled' flag
  10130PLA:PLP:RTS
  10150.L8796:JMP (L0D70)  :\ Old FILEV
  10160.L8799:JMP (L0D72)  :\ Old ARGSV
  10170.L879C:JMP (L0D74)  :\ Old BGETV
  10180.L879F:JMP (L0D76)  :\ Old BPUTV
  10190.L87A2:JMP (L0D78)  :\ Old GBPBV
  10200.L87A5:JMP (L0D7A)  :\ Old FINDV
  10210.L87A8:JMP (L0D7C)  :\ Old FSCV
  10230\ Set/Clear buffer 3 busy flag
  10240\ ----------------------------
  10250.L87AB
  10260ROR &02C6:RTS
  10270:
  10280]:IF O%>L%:PRINT"Code overrun":END
  10290NEXT
  10300OSCLI"Save "+file$+" "+STR$~mcode%+" "+STR$~O%+" 0 FFFBBC00"
  10310OSCLI"Stamp "+file$
  10320ON ERROR ON ERROR OFF:END
  10330*Quit
#+end_src

*** https://mdfs.net/Software/BBC/SROM/Plus1/AP6v134.src

AP6 ROM Manager & Utils v1.34

#+begin_src text
     10REM > AP6v134/src
     20REM Source for Updated PRES AP6 ROM Manager and Utilities
     30REM Can test on Master, but ROM Manager workspace clashes
     40:
     50REM Boilerplate
     60ON ERROR REPORT:PRINT" at line ";ERL:END
     70IF PAGE>&8000:LOADATN "OS_GetEnv"TOA$:IFLEFT$(A$,5)<>"B6502":OSCLI"B6502"+MID$(A$,INSTR(A$," "))
     80A$=MID$(A$,1+INSTR(A$," ",1+INSTR(A$," ",1+INSTR(A$," "))))
     90TARGET$="":IFLEFT$(A$,7)="TARGET=":TARGET$=MID$(A$,8)
    100DEFFNif(A%):IFA%:z%=0:=opt% ELSE z%=P%:=opt%AND-2
    110DEFFNendif:IFz%:z%=P%-z%:P%=P%-z%:O%=O%-z%:=opt% ELSE =opt%
    120DEFFNelse:IFz%:z%=P%-z%:P%=P%-z%:O%=O%-z%:z%=0:=opt% ELSE z%=P%:=opt%AND-2
    130:
    140name$="AP6"
    150DIM mcode% &3000,L% -1:load%=&8000
    160OS_CLI=&FFF7:OSBYTE=&FFF4:OSWORD=&FFF1:OSWRCH=&FFEE
    170OSWRCR=&FFEC:OSNEWL=&FFE7:OSASCI=&FFE3:OSRDCH=&FFE0
    180OSFILE=&FFDD:OSARGS=&FFDA:OSBGET=&FFD7:OSBPUT=&FFD4
    190OSGBPB=&FFD1:OSFIND=&FFCE:GSINIT=&FFC2:GSREAD=&FFC5
    200FSCV=&21E:WHATOS=0
    210:
    220TARGET%=-1:REM Build for 6502Em with no FDC hardware, no real ADFS
    230TARGET%=1 :REM Build for BBC B/B+
    240TARGET%=3 :REM Build for Master
    250TARGET%=5 :REM Build for Compact
    260TARGET%=0 :REM Build for Electron
    270IF TARGET$<>"":TARGET%=VALTARGET$
    280:
    290ver$="1.34":date$="04 Jan 2021"
    300REM              Removed pre-v1.337 code
    310:
    320REM Thoughts: *INSERT/*UNPLUG/*LANG/*TUBE call OSBYTE 162
    330REM OSBYTE 161/162 pass on to any Configure Module before implementing manually
    340REM eg set/reset &DF0,X.b6 to pass on
    350:
    360:
    370REM Base addresses
    380REM --------------
    390IF TARGET%<1              :FDCbase=&FCC4:fdcRES=&20:fdcSIDE=&04:ROMSEL=&FE05:TUBEIO=&FCE0:ROMTABLE=&2A0:FILEBLK=&2E2:REM Electron
    400IF TARGET%=1 OR TARGET%=2 :FDCbase=&FE84:fdcRES=&20:fdcSIDE=&04:ROMSEL=&FE30:TUBEIO=&FEE0:ROMTABLE=&2A1:FILEBLK=&2EE:REM BBC
    410IF TARGET%>2 AND TARGET%<6:FDCbase=&FE28:fdcRES=&04:fdcSIDE=&10:ROMSEL=&FE30:TUBEIO=&FEE0:ROMTABLE=&2A1:FILEBLK=&2ED:REM Master
    420IF TARGET%=0:name$="AP6" ELSE name$="SRAM"
    430:
    440REM Workspace
    450REM ---------
    460LAC=&AC:LAE=&AC:NUM=&AC         :REM Command workspace
    470L0D70=&0D70                     :REM Saved file vectors
    480L0930=&0930                     :REM Base of *FORMAT workspace
    490L09D0=FILEBLK+1:L09EC=FILEBLK+12:REM Base of *VERIFY workspace
    500L0D6C=&0D6C:L0D6D=&0D6D         :REM ROM manager settings
    510L0D6E=&0D6E:L0D6F=&0D6F         :REM UNPLUG bitmap
    520:
    530REM *FORMAT workspace
    540REM -----------------
    550L093B=FILEBLK+0:L0935=FILEBLK+16:L0936=FILEBLK+17
    560L0938=FILEBLK+1:L093C=FILEBLK+2:L093F=FILEBLK+3:L093E=FILEBLK+4
    570L0930=FILEBLK+5:L0932=FILEBLK+6:L0931=FILEBLK+7:L0933=&AB
    580L0939=&AE:L093A=&AF
    590:
    600REM *VERIFY workspace
    610REM -----------------
    620L09ED=L09EC+1:L09EE=L09EC+2:L09EF=L09EC+3
    630L09D1=L09D0+1:L09D2=L09D0+2:L09D3=L09D0+3:L09D4=L09D0+4:L09D5=L09D0+5
    640L09D6=L09D0+6:L09D7=L09D0+7:L09D8=L09D0+8:L09D9=L09D0+9:L09DA=L09D0+&A
    650:
    660REM Workspace
    670REM ---------
    680REM &A8-&AF *commands
    690REM  &AB  =previous NMI owner
    700REM  &AC/D=>formatting data
    710REM  &AE/F=>main memory workspace, verify buffer
    720REM
    730REM &0D68-&0D6C Plus 1 Support
    740REM &0D6D-&0D6F ROM Manager Support
    750REM &0D70-&0D7F ROM/TAPE intercept
    760REM
    770REM &0D6D If b7=0 - default language ROM in b7-b6,b3-b0, b5=TubeDisable, b4=spare
    780REM &0D6E INSERT/UNPLUG bitmap 0-7, b7=7, 0=inserted, 1=unplugged
    790REM &0D6F INSERT/UNPLUG bitmap 8-15, b7=15, 0=inserted, 1=unplugged
    800:
    810:
    820REM I/O addresses
    830REM -------------
    840FDCstatus =FDCbase+0
    850FDCcommand=FDCbase+0
    860FDCtrack  =FDCbase+1
    870FDCsector =FDCbase+2
    880FDCdata   =FDCbase+3
    890FDCdrive  =FDCbase-4
    900:
    910REM &FCC0-08 Floppy disk controller
    920REM  &FCC0 Drive select register
    930REM  &FCC4 FDC command/status
    940REM  &FCC7 FDC data
    950REM
    960REM &FCD8/&FCD9 lock/unlock register bank 5/6
    970REM &FCDA/&FCDB lock/unlock register bank 13/15
    980REM &FCDC/&FCDD lock/unlock register bank 0/2
    990REM &FCDE/&FCDF lock/unlock register bank 1/3
   1000REM
   1010REM &FCFC AQR paging register
   1020REM &FCFD unlock AQR
   1030REM &FCFE lock AQR
   1040:
   1050:
   1060file$=name$+"v"+LEFT$(ver$,1)+MID$(ver$,3,2)+MID$(" BBMEC",TARGET%+1,1)
   1070FOR pass%=0 TO 3
   1080opt%=FNsm_pass(pass%)
   1090[OPT opt%
   1100\ ********************************
   1110\ Updated ROM Management Functions
   1120\ ********************************
   1140.ROMStart
   1150EQUB &00:EQUW RelocTable
   1160JMP ROMMgrServ
   1170EQUB &82:EQUB ROMMgrCopy-ROMStart-1
   1180EQUB EVAL("&"+MID$(ver$,3,2))
   1190EQUS "ROM Manager & Utils":EQUB 0
   1200EQUS LEFT$(ver$,4)+" ("+date$+")":EQUB 0
   1210.ROMMgrCopy
   1220EQUS "(C)J.G.Harston":EQUB 0
   1230.ROMMgrServ
   1240PHA
   1250LDA &0DF0,X:BMI ROMMgrExit :\ Disabled, ignore call
   1260PLA:PHA
   1270CMP #1:BNE P%+5:JMP Serv1
   1280CMP #4:BNE P%+5:JMP Serv4
   1290CMP #7:BNE P%+5:JMP Serv7
   1300CMP #9:BNE P%+5:JMP Serv9
   1310CMP #&10:BNE P%+5:JMP Serv10
   1320.ROMMgrExit
   1330PLA:RTS
   1360\ Generate inline error
   1370\ =====================
   1380.MkError
   1390PLA:STA &FD:PLA:STA &FE
   1400LDY #0
   1410.MkErrorLp
   1420INY:LDA (&FD),Y:STA &100,Y
   1430BNE MkErrorLp
   1440STA &100:JMP &100
   1470\ Print inline text
   1480\ =================
   1490\ Corrupts A,X,Y
   1500\
   1510.PrText
   1520PLA:STA &F6:PLA:STA &F7:LDY #1
   1530.PrTextLp
   1540LDA (&F6),Y:BEQ PrTextEnd
   1550JSR OSASCI:INY:BNE PrTextLp
   1560.PrTextEnd
   1570CLC:TYA:ADC &F6:TAY
   1580LDA #0:ADC &F7:PHA
   1590TYA:PHA:RTS
   1610\ Print character in Y if MI set, else space
   1620\ ------------------------------------------
   1630.PrCharY
   1640BPL PrSpace               :\ MI not set, print a space
   1650TYA:JMP OSWRCH            :\ MI set, print the character
   1670\ Print spaces
   1680\ ============
   1690.Pr2Spaces
   1700JSR PrSpace
   1710.PrSpace
   1720LDA #ASC" ":JMP OSASCI
   1740\ Print line number in &A8/9
   1750\ ==========================
   1760.PrLineNumSpc
   1770JSR PrLineNum:BCC PrSpace
   1780.PrLineNum
   1790SED:CLC
   1800LDA &A8:ADC #&01:STA &A8  :\ Increment &A8/A9 in decimal mode
   1810.LBFE4
   1820LDA &A9:ADC #&00:STA &A9
   1830CLD:JSR PrHex             :\ Print high byte
   1840LDA &A8                   :\ Print low byte
   1850:
   1860\ Print hex number in A
   1870\ =====================
   1880.PrHex
   1890PHA:LSR A:LSR A:LSR A
   1900LSR A:JSR PrNybble:PLA
   1910.PrNybble
   1920AND #&0F:CMP #&0A
   1930BCC PrDigit:ADC #&06
   1940.PrDigit
   1950ADC #&30:JMP OSWRCH
   1970\ Print validated character
   1980\ =========================
   1990.PrChar
   2000AND #&7F
   2010CMP #&20:BCC X8C71
   2020CMP #&7F:BCC X8C73
   2030.X8C71
   2040LDA #ASC"."
   2050.X8C73
   2060JMP OSWRCH
   2090\ Skip spaces
   2100\ ===========
   2110\ Returns A=non-space character
   2120\         CS=more parameters
   2130\         CC=no parameters, at <cr>
   2140.SkipSpaces1
   2150INY
   2160.SkipSpaces
   2170LDA (&F2),Y:CMP #ASC" "
   2180BEQ SkipSpaces1:RTS
   2210\ Parse number
   2220\ ============
   2230\ Parse "" * or <rom>
   2240\ -------------------
   2250.L8CA0
   2260LDA (&F2),Y
   2270CMP #ASC"*":BEQ ChkDigitCC      :\ EQ+CC='*'
   2280:
   2290\ Parse "" or <rom>
   2300\ -------------------
   2310.L8CA5
   2320LDA (&F2),Y
   2330CMP #13:BEQ ChkDigitCS          :\ EQ+CS=<cr>
   2340:
   2350\ Parse 4-bit <rom> hex 0-F or dec 0-15
   2360\ -------------------------------------
   2370.ScanDec4
   2380JSR ScanDec8
   2390CMP #&10:BCS errBadNumber       :\ Too big
   2400RTS                             :\ NE+CC=<num>
   2410:
   2420\ Parse 8-bit <num>
   2430\ -----------------
   2440.ScanDec8
   2450JSR CheckDigit:BCS errBadNumber
   2460STA NUM                         :\ Store as current number
   2470CMP #10:BCS ScanDecDone         :\ Hex number, exit
   2480.ScanDecLp
   2490JSR ScanDigit:BCS ScanDecDone   :\ No more digits
   2500PHA                             :\ Save digit
   2510LDA NUM:CMP #26:BCS errBadNumber:\ num>25, num*25>255
   2520ASL A:ASL A:ADC NUM:ASL A       :\ num=num*10
   2530STA NUM:PLA:ADC NUM:STA NUM     :\ num=num*10+digit
   2540BCC ScanDecLp                   :\ Loop for next digit
   2550.errBadNumber
   2560JSR MkError:EQUB 252:EQUS "Bad number":BRK
   2570.ScanDecDone
   2580LDA (&F2),Y
   2590CMP #ASC"!":BCS errBadNumber    :\ Badly terminated number
   2600LDA NUM
   2610.ChkDigitCS
   2620SEC:RTS                         :\ Return A=number, CS
   2630:
   2640.CheckDigit
   2650LDA (&F2),Y
   2660CMP #ASC"A":BCC ScanDigit2      :\ Decimal digit
   2670AND #&DF
   2680CMP #ASC"F"+1:BCS ChkDigitCS    :\ >'F'
   2690SBC #6:BNE ScanDigit3           :\ Convert 'A'-'F'
   2700.ScanDigit
   2710LDA (&F2),Y
   2720.ScanDigit2
   2730CMP #ASC"0":BCC ChkDigitCS      :\ <'0'
   2740CMP #ASC"9"+1:BCS ChkDigitCS    :\ >'9'
   2750.ScanDigit3
   2760INY:AND #&0F
   2770.ChkDigitCC
   2780CLC:RTS                         :\ Return A=digit, CC
   2810\ SERVICE 1 - Do Reset processing
   2820\ ===============================
   2830.Serv1
   2840TYA:PHA                    :\ Save current workspace address
   2850OPT FNif(TARGET%<3)
   2860  LDA L0D6D:ASL A:ASL A    :\ Get Tube Enable from bit 5
   2870  BPL Serv1a               :\ bit 5 = 0, not disabled
   2880  LDA #0:STA &027A         :\ Disable Tube
   2890  LDA &FFB7:STA &A8        :\ Point to default vectors
   2900  LDA &FFB8:STA &A9        :\ Reset EVENTV and BRKV
   2910  LDY #&21                 :\ Point to EVENTV
   2920  .Serv1lp
   2930  LDA (&A8),Y:STA &200,Y
   2940  CPY #&20:BNE P%+4:LDY #4 :\ EVENTV done, point to BRKV+1
   2950  DEY:CPY #1:BNE Serv1lp
   2960OPT FNendif
   2970.Serv1a
   2980LDA &028D
   2990CMP #&01:BNE Serv1Done   :\ Check last Break type
   3000LDY #1:JSR LockUnlockAll :\ If Power on, lock (write protect) all ROMs
   3010.Serv1Done
   3020PLA:TAY
   3030.Serv1Exit
   3040PLA:RTS
   3070\ SERVICE 7 - Read/Write configuration settings
   3080\ =============================================
   3090.Serv7
   3100LDA &EF
   3110CMP #&A1:BEQ Serv7go     :\ OSBYTE &A1 - Read settings
   3120CMP #&A2:BNE Serv1Exit   :\ OSBYTE &A2 - Write settings
   3130.Serv7go
   3140LDX &F0
   3150CPX #15:BEQ Serv7Tube    :\ Location 15, Tube On/Off
   3160CPX #5:BCC Serv1Exit
   3170CPX #8:BCS Serv1Exit
   3180\  5 = b4-b7=LANG
   3190\  6 = b0-b7=unplug bitmap 0-7
   3200\  7 = b0-b7=unplug bitmap 8-15
   3210\ 15 = b0, 0=NoTube, 1=Tube
   3220LSR A:BCC Serv7Write
   3230LDA L0D6D-5,X            :\ Read configuration setting
   3240CPX #5:BNE Serv7RdOk     :\ Return unplug bitmap
   3250ASL A:ASL A:ASL A:ASL A  :\ Move LANG to b4-b7
   3260EOR #&FF
   3270.Serv7RdOk
   3280EOR #&FF:TAY             :\ Return in Y
   3290.Serv7Done
   3300PLA:LDA #0:RTS           :\ Claim and return
   3310.Serv7Write
   3320TYA:EOR #&FF             :\ Toggle unplug bitmap
   3330CPX #5:BNE Serv7WrOk
   3340LSR A:LSR A:LSR A:LSR A  :\ A=0000llll
   3350EOR L0D6D                :\ A=xxTxWWWW
   3360AND #&0F                 :\ A=0000WWWW
   3370EOR L0D6D                :\ A=xxTxllll
   3380EOR #&0F                 :\ A=xxTxLLLL
   3390.Serv7WrOk
   3400STA L0D6D-5,X:JMP Serv7Done
   3410.Serv7Tube
   3420LSR A:BCS Serv7TubeRd    :\ Jump with Read
   3430.Serv7TubeWr
   3440TYA:LSR A                :\ Cy=~T
   3450LDA L0D6D                :\ A=xxTxLLLL
   3460AND #&DF                 :\ A=xx0xLLLL
   3470BCS Serv7WrOk            :\ Enable Tube
   3480ORA #&20:BNE Serv7WrOk   :\ Disable Tube
   3490.Serv7TubeRd
   3500LDA L0D6D                :\ A=xxTxLLLL
   3510AND #&20:CMP #&20        :\ A=00Tx0000
   3520LDA #&FE:ADC #0          :\ A=1111111T
   3530JMP Serv7RdOk            :\ EOR FF and return
   3560\ SERVICE &10 - Spool/Exec closing
   3570\ ================================
   3580\ Insert/Unplug ROMs
   3590\ Needs to be done before Serv1, on BBC/Elk Serv10 before Serv01
   3600\ Ctrl-CAPS check needs to be done here to enable ROM before Serv01
   3610\ Check Ctrl-* and jump to *-prompt
   3620\
   3630.Serv10
   3640LDA &EF:ORA &F0
   3650ORA &F1:BNE Serv10Exit   :\ Not BREAK, skip
   3660LDA &028D
   3670CMP #&01:BNE X813E       :\ Not Power-On, insert/unplug ROMs
   3680OPT FNif(TARGET%<3)
   3690  \ &024B=00xxrrrr - 6502 BASIC ROM
   3700  \      =01xxrrrr - 6502 BASIC don't relocate
   3710  \      =10xxrrrr - non-6502 BASIC ROM
   3720  \      =11xxxxxx - no BASIC
   3730  LDA &024B              :\ Get BASIC ROM number
   3740  .Serv10SetLang
   3750  EOR L0D6D:AND #&CF     :\ Clear and merge LANG bits
   3760  EOR L0D6D:STA L0D6D    :\ Default language = BASIC
   3770OPT FNendif
   3780PLA:RTS
   3790:
   3800.X813E
   3810LDX #15:LDA L0D6F        :\ Unplug bitmap for ROMs 8-15
   3820.Serv10Lp1
   3830CPX #7:BNE Serv10a
   3840LDA L0D6E                :\ Unplug bitmap for ROMs 0-7
   3850.Serv10a
   3860ROL A:BCC Serv10Next           :\ Leave inserted
   3870PHA:LDA #0:STA ROMTABLE,X:PLA  :\ Remove from ROM table
   3880.Serv10Next
   3890DEX:BPL Serv10Lp1        :\ Loop down to ROM 0
   3900\.Serv10Done
   3910LDA #&7A:JSR OSBYTE       :\ Check keys pressed
   3920CPX #&48:BEQ Serv10Enable :\ *
   3930CPX #&5B:BEQ Serv10Enable :\ K*
   3940CPX #&40:BNE Serv10Exit   :\ CAPSLOCK
   3950CLC                       :\ CLC=CAPSLOCK
   3960.Serv10Enable
   3970PHP:LDA #&78:JSR OSBYTE   :\ Clear keypressed info
   3980LDA &8006
   3990LDX &F4:STA ROMTABLE,X    :\ Re-enable myself
   4000PLP:BCC Serv10Exit
   4010JSR MkError:EQUB 249:EQUS "Supervisor":BRK
   4020.Serv10Exit
   4030PLA:RTS
   4060\ SERVICE 4 - *command
   4070\ ====================
   4080\ Only ever passed commands starting with a letter
   4090.Serv4
   4100LDA (&F2),Y:AND #&DF
   4110CMP #ASC"P":BNE Serv4Go     :\ Command prefixed by 'P'?
   4120INY:JSR Serv4Prefix:DEY     :\ Check for prefixed command
   4130TAX:BNE Serv10Exit          :\ Not recognised, return unclaimed
   4140PLA:TXA:RTS                 :\ Recognised, claim and return
   4150.Serv4Prefix
   4160PHA
   4170.Serv4Go
   4180LDX #cmdFILE-cmdBase        :\ Point to command table
   4190.Serv4a
   4200TYA:PHA                     :\ Save start of command line
   4210.CmdLookNext
   4220PLA:PHA:TAY                 :\ Get text offset back
   4230.CmdLookLp
   4240LDA (&F2),Y:CMP #ASC".":BEQ CmdMatchDot
   4250CMP cmdBase,X:BEQ CmdLook2
   4260AND #&DF:CMP cmdBase,X:BNE CmdNoMatch
   4270.CmdLook2
   4280INX:INY                     :\ Step to next character
   4290LDA cmdBase,X:BPL CmdLookLp :\ Not a full command
   4300LDA (&F2),Y                 :\ Get following character
   4310CMP #ASC"A":BCS CmdCheckNext:\ Ensure command ends with non-alpha
   4320.CmdFoundSpc
   4330JSR SkipSpaces              :\ Move past any spaces
   4340LDA cmdBase,X:ASL A:TAX     :\ Get command byte, index into addresses
   4350PLA:STA &F5:PLA             :\ Get saved Y and A
   4360CMP #9:BEQ HelpDispatch     :\ Do *Help
   4370JSR CmdDispatch:LDA #0:RTS  :\ Call command, claim call
   4380.CmdDispatch
   4390LDA cmdAddrs-2+1,X:PHA      :\ Get command address
   4400LDA cmdAddrs-2+0,X:PHA
   4410LDA #0:RTS                  :\ Jump to routine via stack
   4420\ On entry to routines,
   4430\  A=0, (&F2),Y=>command line, skipped past any spaces
   4440:
   4450.CmdMatchDot
   4460LDA cmdBase,X               :\ Is there still more name to match?
   4470BMI CmdCheckNext            :\ End of name, the dot is after end of command name
   4480INY                         :\ Step past '.'
   4490.CmdMatchDotLp
   4500INX:LDA cmdBase,X
   4510BPL CmdMatchDotLp           :\ Find end of table entry
   4520BMI CmdFoundSpc             :\ Jump to enter command
   4530:
   4540.CmdNoMatch
   4550INX:LDA cmdBase,X
   4560BPL CmdNoMatch              :\ Find end of table entry
   4570.CmdCheckNext
   4580INX:LDA cmdBase,X
   4590BPL CmdLookNext             :\ Loop until table terminator found
   4600INX:ASL A:BPL CmdLookNext   :\ Not end of final table
   4610JMP ServQuit
   4640\ SERVICE 9 - *Help
   4650\ =================
   4660.Serv9
   4670LDA (&F2),Y:CMP #13:BEQ HelpShort
   4680CMP #ASC".":BEQ HelpLong
   4690LDX #cmdHELP-cmdBase
   4700JMP Serv4a
   4710.HelpShort
   4720TYA:PHA
   4730SEC:JSR PrintHelpTitleLong
   4740LDX #cmdHELP-cmdBase
   4750LDY #hlpHELP-hlpBase
   4760JSR HelpLp1
   4770.ServQuit
   4780PLA:TAY:LDX &F4:PLA:RTS
   4790:
   4800.HelpDispatch
   4810PHA:LDA &F5:PHA          :\ Save Y and A again
   4820LDY cmdAddrs-2+1,X       :\ Offset to help strings
   4830LDA cmdAddrs-2+0,X:TAX   :\ Offset to command strings
   4840BEQ HelpSpecial
   4850JSR HelpPrint
   4860JMP ServQuit
   4870:
   4880.HelpLong
   4890TYA:PHA
   4900LDX #cmdFILE-cmdBase:LDY #hlpFILE-hlpBase:JSR HelpPrint
   4910LDX #cmdSRAM-cmdBase:LDY #hlpSRAM-hlpBase:JSR HelpPrint
   4920JMP ServQuit
   4930:
   4940\ *Help +1
   4950.HelpSpecial
   4960SEC:JSR PrintHelpTitleLong
   4970LDX #0
   4980.HelpPlus1Lp
   4990TXA:PHA
   5000LDA idxPlus1+1,X:TAY
   5010LDA idxPlus1+0,X:TAX
   5020JSR HelpPrCmd
   5030PLA:TAX:INX:INX
   5040LDA idxPlus1,X
   5050BNE HelpPlus1Lp
   5060BEQ ServQuit
   5070:
   5080.HelpPrint
   5090JSR PrintHelpTitle
   5100JSR OSNEWL
   5110.HelpLp1
   5120JSR HelpPrCmd       :\ Print this command string
   5130INX:LDA cmdBase,X   :\ Get first byte from next
   5140BPL HelpLp1:RTS     :\ Loop if not terminator
   5150:
   5160.HelpPrCmd
   5170JSR Pr2Spaces
   5180.HelpPrLp1
   5190LDA cmdBase,X:BMI HelpPrSyntax
   5200JSR OSWRCH:INX:BNE HelpPrLp1
   5210.HelpPrSyntax
   5220.HelpPrLpSpc
   5230JSR PrSpace
   5240SEC:LDA &318:SBC &308:SBC #12
   5250BCC HelpPrLpSpc
   5260.HelpLp2
   5270LDA hlpBase,Y:JSR OSASCI
   5280INY:CMP #13:BNE HelpLp2
   5290RTS
   5300:
   5310.PrintHelpTitle
   5320CLC
   5330.PrintHelpTitleLong
   5340TYA:PHA:TXA:PHA
   5350PHP:JSR PrText:EQUB 13
   5360OPT FNif(TARGET%=0)
   5370  EQUS "RetroHardware Plus 1 Support "+LEFT$(ver$,4)+LEFT$(CHR$(96+VALRIGHT$(ver$,1)),LENver$>4)
   5380OPT FNelse
   5390  EQUS "ROM Manager and Utilities "+LEFT$(ver$,4)+LEFT$(CHR$(96+VALRIGHT$(ver$,1)),LENver$>4)
   5400OPT FNendif
   5410EQUB 0
   5420PLP:BCC PrHelpTitleDone
   5430OPT FNif(TARGET%=0)
   5440  LDA &30A:SBC &308:SBC #47:BCC PrHelpTitleDone1
   5450  JSR PrText
   5460  EQUS " (ADC/Printer/RS423)"
   5470  EQUB 0
   5480OPT FNendif
   5490.PrHelpTitleDone1
   5500JSR OSNEWL
   5510.PrHelpTitleDone
   5520PLA:TAX:PLA:TAY
   5530RTS
   5560\ Command tables
   5570\ --------------
   5580\ *Help lists until bit 7 set
   5590\ *command lookup searches until &FF terminator
   5600\ So, commands between &80 and &FF are executed but not listed
   5610\
   5620.cmdBase
   5630.cmdHELP
   5640EQUS "+1"   :EQUB &81
   5650EQUS "SRAM" :EQUB &82
   5660EQUS "UTILS":EQUB &83
   5670EQUB &80
   5680EQUS "SROM" :EQUB &82
   5690EQUB &FF
   5700:
   5710.cmdFILE
   5720.cmdBUILD   :EQUS "BUILD"   :EQUB &84
   5730.cmdDUMP    :EQUS "DUMP"    :EQUB &85
   5740.cmdFORMAT  :EQUS "FORMAT"  :EQUB &86
   5750.cmdLIST    :EQUS "LIST"    :EQUB &87
   5760.cmdTUBE    :EQUS "TUBE"    :EQUB &88
   5770.cmdTYPE    :EQUS "TYPE"    :EQUB &89
   5780.cmdVERIFY  :EQUS "VERIFY"  :EQUB &8A
   5790EQUB &80
   5800:
   5810.cmdSRAM
   5820.cmdAQRPAGE :EQUS "AQRPAGE" :EQUB &97
   5830.cmdINSERT  :EQUS "INSERT"  :EQUB &8B
   5840.cmdLANG    :EQUS "LANG"    :EQUB &8C
   5850.cmdLROMS   :EQUS "LROMS"   :EQUB &8D
   5860.cmdROMS    :EQUS "ROMS"    :EQUB &8E
   5870.cmdSRLOAD  :EQUS "SRLOAD"  :EQUB &90
   5880.cmdSRLOCK  :EQUS "SRLOCK"  :EQUB &91
   5890.cmdSRPAGE  :EQUS "SRPAGE"  :EQUB &8F
   5900.cmdSRSAVE  :EQUS "SRSAVE"  :EQUB &92
   5910.cmdSRUNLOCK:EQUS "SRUNLOCK":EQUB &93
   5920.cmdSRWIPE  :EQUS "SRWIPE"  :EQUB &94
   5930.cmdUNPLUG  :EQUS "UNPLUG"  :EQUB &95
   5940.cmdUROMS   :EQUS "UROMS"   :EQUB &96
   5950EQUB &80
   5960:
   5970.cmdPlus1
   5980.cmdLOADROM :EQUS "LOADROM":EQUB &90
   5990.cmdLOCK    :EQUS "LOCK"   :EQUB &91
   6000.cmdRLOAD   :EQUS "RLOAD"  :EQUB &90
   6010.cmdRSAVE   :EQUS "RSAVE"  :EQUB &92
   6020.cmdSAVEROM :EQUS "SAVEROM":EQUB &92
   6030.cmdUNLOCK  :EQUS "UNLOCK" :EQUB &93
   6040.cmdZERO    :EQUS "ZERO"   :EQUB &94
   6050EQUB &FF
   6060]:A%=P%-cmdBase:IF A%>255:IF (pass%AND3)=0:PRINT"WARNING: Command table ";A%-255;" bytes too long"
   6070[OPT opt%
   6080:
   6090\ Command help strings
   6100\ --------------------
   6110.hlpBase
   6120.hlpFILE
   6130.hlpBUILD   :EQUS "<file>":EQUB 13
   6140.hlpDUMP    :EQUS "<file>":EQUB 13
   6150.hlpFORMAT  :EQUS "<drive> <S|M|L> (Y)":EQUB 13
   6160.hlpLIST    :EQUS "<file>":EQUB 13
   6170.hlpTUBE    :EQUS "ON|OFF|<num>":EQUB 13
   6180.hlpTYPE    :EQUS "<file>":EQUB 13
   6190.hlpVERIFY  :EQUS "<drive>":EQUB 13
   6200:
   6210.hlpSRAM
   6220.hlpAQRPAGE :EQUS "<page>":EQUB 13
   6230.hlpINSERT  :EQUS "(<rom> (I))":EQUB 13
   6240.hlpLANG    :EQUS "(<rom>)":EQUB 13
   6250.hlpLROMS   :EQUS "":EQUB 13
   6260.hlpROMS    :EQUS "":EQUB 13
   6270.hlpSRLOAD  :EQUS "<file> <rom> (U)(L)(I)":EQUB 13
   6280.hlpSRLOCK  :EQUS "(<rom>)":EQUB 13
   6290.hlpSRPAGE  :EQUS "<rom> <page> (I)":EQUB 13
   6300.hlpSRSAVE  :EQUS "<file> <rom>":EQUB 13
   6310.hlpSRUNLOCK:EQUS "(<rom>)":EQUB 13
   6320.hlpSRWIPE  :EQUS "<rom> (U)(L)":EQUB 13
   6330.hlpUNPLUG  :EQUS "(<rom> (U))":EQUB 13
   6340.hlpUROMS   :EQUS "":EQUB 13
   6350.hlpHELP    :EQUB 13:EQUB 13:EQUB 13
   6360]:A%=P%-hlpBase:IF A%>255:IF (pass%AND3)=0:PRINT"WARNING: Help table ";A%-255;" bytes too long"
   6370[OPT opt%
   6380:
   6390\ Command dispatch addresses
   6400\ --------------------------
   6410.cmdAddrs
   6420EQUW 0                                    :\ +1    &81
   6430EQUB cmdSRAM-cmdBase:EQUB hlpSRAM-hlpBase :\ SRAM  &82
   6440EQUB cmdFILE-cmdBase:EQUB hlpFILE-hlpBase :\ UTILS &83
   6450EQUW BUILD-1    :\ &84
   6460EQUW DUMP-1     :\ &85
   6470EQUW AFORMAT-1  :\ &86
   6480EQUW FLIST-1    :\ &87
   6490EQUW TUBE-1     :\ &88
   6500EQUW TYPE-1     :\ &89
   6510EQUW VERIFY-1   :\ &8A
   6520EQUW INSERT-1   :\ &8B
   6530EQUW LANG-1     :\ &8C
   6540EQUW LROMS-1    :\ &8D
   6550EQUW ROMS-1     :\ &8E
   6560EQUW SRPAGE-1   :\ &8F
   6570EQUW SRLOAD-1   :\ &90
   6580EQUW SRLOCK-1   :\ &91
   6590EQUW SRSAVE-1   :\ &92
   6600EQUW SRUNLOCK-1 :\ &93
   6610EQUW SRWIPE-1   :\ &94
   6620EQUW UNPLUG-1   :\ &95
   6630EQUW UROMS-1    :\ &96
   6640EQUW AQRPAGE-1  :\ &97
   6650:
   6660\ *Help +1 offsets
   6670\ ----------------
   6680.idxPlus1
   6690EQUB cmdAQRPAGE-cmdBase:EQUB hlpAQRPAGE -hlpBase
   6700EQUB cmdBUILD  -cmdBase:EQUB hlpBUILD   -hlpBase
   6710EQUB cmdDUMP   -cmdBase:EQUB hlpDUMP    -hlpBase
   6720EQUB cmdFORMAT -cmdBase:EQUB hlpFORMAT  -hlpBase
   6730EQUB cmdINSERT -cmdBase:EQUB hlpINSERT  -hlpBase
   6740EQUB cmdLANG   -cmdBase:EQUB hlpLANG    -hlpBase
   6750EQUB cmdLIST   -cmdBase:EQUB hlpLIST    -hlpBase
   6760EQUB cmdLOADROM-cmdBase:EQUB hlpSRLOAD  -hlpBase
   6770EQUB cmdLOCK   -cmdBase:EQUB hlpSRLOCK  -hlpBase
   6780EQUB cmdLROMS  -cmdBase:EQUB hlpLROMS   -hlpBase
   6790EQUB cmdRLOAD  -cmdBase:EQUB hlpSRLOAD  -hlpBase
   6800EQUB cmdRSAVE  -cmdBase:EQUB hlpSRSAVE  -hlpBase
   6810EQUB cmdROMS   -cmdBase:EQUB hlpROMS    -hlpBase
   6820EQUB cmdSAVEROM-cmdBase:EQUB hlpSRSAVE  -hlpBase
   6830EQUB cmdSRPAGE -cmdBase:EQUB hlpSRPAGE  -hlpBase
   6840EQUB cmdTUBE   -cmdBase:EQUB hlpTUBE    -hlpBase
   6850EQUB cmdTYPE   -cmdBase:EQUB hlpTYPE    -hlpBase
   6860EQUB cmdUNLOCK -cmdBase:EQUB hlpSRUNLOCK-hlpBase
   6870EQUB cmdUNPLUG -cmdBase:EQUB hlpUNPLUG  -hlpBase
   6880EQUB cmdUROMS  -cmdBase:EQUB hlpUROMS   -hlpBase
   6890EQUB cmdVERIFY -cmdBase:EQUB hlpVERIFY  -hlpBase
   6900EQUB cmdZERO   -cmdBase:EQUB hlpSRWIPE  -hlpBase
   6910EQUB &00
   6940\ *************
   6950\ FILE COMMANDS
   6960\ *************
   6980\ Workspace for UTILS commands
   6990\  &A8-&AF = 'MOUNT x'<cr> command
   7000\  &A8/9=line number
   7010\  &AA  =CR/LF flag, BUILD handle
   7020\  &AB  =type/list flag, dump EOF,   OSWORD 0 addr.lo
   7030\  &AC  =                dump count, OSWORD 0 addr.hi
   7040\  &AD                   dump width, OSWORD 0 length
   7050\  &AE                               OSWORD 0 min
   7060\  &AF                               OSWORD 0 max
   7070\
   7080\ *DELETEABS <file>
   7090\ ============
   7100.FLIST
   7110LDA #&FF                   :\ &FF=DELETEABS
   7130\ *TYPE <file>
   7140\ ============
   7150.TYPE                      :\ Entered with &00=TYPE
   7160STA &AB:JSR OpeninFile     :\ Save list type, open file
   7170LDX #0:BEQ type7           :\ Jump into loop
   7180:
   7190\ Type/List characters from file
   7200\ ------------------------------
   7210.type1
   7220CLC:PHP:TAX:JSR OSWRCH:PLP
   7230.type2
   7240PHP:JSR OSBGET:BCS typeEOF :\ End of file
   7250BIT &FF:BMI LBEED          :\ Escape pressed
   7260PLP:BCC type3
   7270PHA:JSR PrLineNumSpc:PLA
   7280.type3
   7290CMP #10:BEQ type4:CMP #13:BNE type1
   7300.type4
   7310CPX #10:BEQ type5:CPX #13:BNE type6
   7320.type5
   7330STX &AA:CMP &AA:CLC:BNE type2
   7340.type6
   7350TAX:JSR OSNEWL
   7360.type7
   7370LDA &AB:LSR A:BPL type2
   7380.typeEOF
   7390LDA &318:BEQ type10:JSR OSNEWL
   7400.type10
   7410PLP
   7420.LBEDA
   7430LDA #&00:JMP OSFIND
   7460\ *DUMP <filename>
   7470\ ================
   7480.DUMP
   7490JSR OpeninFile
   7500LDA &30A:SBC &308        :\ Text window width
   7510SBC #4:LSR A:LSR A:AND #&1C
   7520CMP #4:BCS P%+4:LDA #3
   7530STA &AD
   7540:
   7550\ Dump a line from file
   7560\ ---------------------
   7570.LBEED
   7580BIT &FF:BMI LBF56        :\ Escape pressed, tidy up and quit
   7590CLC:JSR LBFE4            :\ Print file offset
   7600JSR PrSpace              :\ Print space
   7610TSX:TXA:SEC:SBC &AD:TAX:TXS :\ Claim bytes on stack
   7620LDA &AD:STA &AC             :\ Number of bytes to dump
   7630:
   7640\ Dump 8 bytes as hex
   7650\ -------------------
   7660.LBF04
   7670JSR OSBGET:BCS LBF16
   7680STA &101,X
   7690JSR PrHex:JSR PrSpace    :\ Print hex, space
   7700INX:DEC &AC:BNE LBF04
   7710CLC:BEQ LBF2C
   7720:
   7730\ EOF met, pad with '**'
   7740\ ----------------------
   7750.LBF16
   7760LDA #ASC"*":JSR OSWRCH
   7770JSR OSWRCH:JSR PrSpace
   7780LDA #&00:STA &101,X
   7790INX:DEC &AC:BNE LBF16
   7800SEC
   7810:
   7820\ Dump as characters
   7830\ ------------------
   7840.LBF2C
   7850ROR &AB                :\ Save EOF flag in bit 7
   7860LDX &AD:TXA            :\ Number of bytes to display
   7870CLC:ADC &A8:STA &A8    :\ Update offset
   7880BCC LBF30:INC &A9
   7890.LBF30
   7900PLA:JSR PrChar         :\ Print printable character
   7910DEX:BNE LBF30
   7920JSR OSNEWL
   7930ROL &AB                :\ Check EOF flag
   7940.DumpEOF
   7950BCS LBEDA              :\ Close and finish
   7960TYA:PHA:TAX            :\ Save handle, X=handle
   7970LDA #127:JSR OSBYTE    :\ Read EOF
   7980PLA:TAY                :\ Get handle back
   7990CPX #1:BCC LBEED       :\ Not at EOF, loop for more
   8000BCS DumpEOF            :\ At EOF, close and finish
   8010:
   8020\ Escape pressed
   8030\ --------------
   8040.LBF56
   8050JSR LBEDA       :\ Close file
   8060.errEscape
   8070JSR L97B2       :\ Acknowledge Escape
   8080JSR MkError:EQUB 17:EQUS "Escape":BRK
   8110\ *BUILD <file>
   8120\ =============
   8130.BUILD
   8140LDA #&80:JSR OpenFile  :\ Open file for output
   8150STA &AA
   8160:
   8170\ Get a line of input
   8180\ -------------------
   8190.LBF69
   8200JSR PrLineNumSpc
   8210LDA #ASC" ":STA &AE
   8220TSX:TXA:SBC #&40:STA &AD
   8230LDY #&FF:STY &AF
   8240INY:STY &AB
   8250INY:STY &AC
   8260DEY:TYA:LDX #&AB
   8270JSR OSWORD:BCS LBF56
   8280LDX #0:LDY &AA
   8290.LBF8E
   8300LDA &100,X:JSR OSBPUT
   8310INX:CMP #13:BNE LBF8E
   8320BEQ LBF69
   8330:
   8340.OpeninFile
   8350LDA #&40                 :\ &40=OPENIN
   8360.OpenFile
   8370PHA
   8380LDA #&00:STA &A8:STA &A9 :\ Line number=0
   8390TYA:CLC:ADC &F2:TAX      :\ Convert (&F2),Y to XY
   8400LDA &F3:ADC #&00:TAY
   8410PLA:JSR OSFIND           :\ Open file
   8420TAY:BNE OpenFileOk       :\ File found
   8430.errFileNotFound
   8440JSR MkError:EQUB 214:EQUS "File not found":BRK
   8470\ *TUBE ON|OFF|<num>
   8480\ ==================
   8490.TUBE
   8500LDA (&F2),Y:AND #&DF     :\ Get first character
   8510CMP #ASC"O":BNE TubeNum  :\ Not *TUBE O, try for number
   8520INY
   8530LDA (&F2),Y:AND #&DF     :\ Get upper case
   8540CMP #ASC"N":BEQ TubeOn   :\ *TUBE ON
   8550CMP #ASC"F":BEQ TubeOff  :\ *TUBE OFF
   8560DEY                      :\ Step back to error with non-number
   8570.TubeNum
   8580JSR ScanDec8             :\ Scan for number
   8590OPT FNif(TARGET%<3)
   8600  STA TUBEIO+6                    :\ Select Tube CPU on Electron or BBC
   8610OPT FNendif
   8620OPT FNif(TARGET%>2)
   8630  TAX:PHP:SEI:LDA &FE34           :\ Disable IRQs, get current Tube
   8640  EOR #&10:STA &FE34:STX TUBEIO+6 :\ Swap Tube, select Tube CPU
   8650  EOR #&10:STA &FE34:STX TUBEIO+6 :\ Swap Tube back, select Tube CPU
   8660  PLP                             :\ Restore IRQs
   8670OPT FNendif
   8680RTS
   8690.TubeOn
   8700.TubeOff
   8710EOR #8:AND #8:ASL A:ASL A :\ Move ON/OFF to bit 5
   8720EOR L0D6D:AND #&20        :\ Clear and merge TUBE bit
   8730EOR L0D6D:STA L0D6D       :\ Should only do on non-Master
   8740RTS
   8770\ ********************
   8780\ ROM Manager Commands
   8790\ ********************
   8810\ *LROMS (*)
   8820\ ==========
   8830.LROMS
   8840LDA #1                :\ A=1 to lock
   8850:
   8860\ *UROMS (*)
   8870\ ==========
   8880.UROMS                :\ Entered with A=0 to unlock
   8890TAX:TYA:PHA:TXA
   8900TAY:JSR LockUnlockAll :\ Lock/unlock all ROMs
   8910PLA:TAY               :\ Drop through to *ROMS
   8920:
   8930\ *ROMS (*)
   8940\ =========
   8950.ROMS
   8960LDA (&F2),Y:CMP #ASC"*"
   8970BEQ P%+3:CLC          :\ SEC=all ROMs
   8980LDX #&0F:STX &F5      :\ Start at ROM 15
   8990.L8C22
   9000PHP
   9010LDX &F5:JSR PrRomInfoAll :\ Display info for ROM X
   9020BCS L8C2D             :\ Skip if nothing displayed
   9030LDA #8:JSR OSWRCH     :\ Prevent line wrapping
   9040JSR OSNEWL
   9050.L8C2D
   9060PLP:DEC &F5:BPL L8C22 :\ Loop for all ROMs
   9070.OpenFileOk
   9080RTS
   9110\ *LANG (<rom>)
   9120\ =============
   9130\ Updates:
   9140\ Doesn't enter selected language, just configures it.
   9150\ If no <rom>, no default, allows MOS to select default
   9160.LANG
   9170JSR L8CA5              :\ Scan for ROM number
   9180BCC P%+4:LDA #&FF      :\ If no <rom>, set no default
   9190PHA:JMP Serv10SetLang  :\ Set as default language
   9220\ Is ROM X unplugged?
   9230\ -------------------
   9240\ On exit, CC=ROM is inserted
   9250\          CS=ROM is unplugged
   9260.L8D07
   9270JSR XtoBitmap:BCS L8D1A :\ Jump for ROM 8-15
   9280AND L0D6E:BCC L8D1D     :\ Mask with bitmap for 0-7
   9290.L8D1A
   9300AND L0D6F               :\ Mask with bitmap for 8-15
   9310.L8D1D
   9320CMP #1:RTS              :\ CC=inserted, CS=unplugged
   9330.XtoBitmap
   9340TXA:CMP #8:AND #7:TAX
   9350LDA Bitmap,X:RTS
   9360.Bitmap
   9370EQUB &01:EQUB &02:EQUB &04:EQUB &08
   9380EQUB &10:EQUB &20:EQUB &40:EQUB &80
   9410\ *SRPAGE <rom> <page> (I)(U)(L)
   9420\ ==============================
   9430\ Lock/Unlock doesn't make sense, as <rom> must be ROM anyway
   9440\
   9450.SRPAGE
   9460JSR ScanDec4:STA &F5   :\ Scan for a short hex number
   9470JSR SkipSpaces
   9480JSR ScanDec4:PHA       :\ Scan for another short hex number
   9490JSR srOptionsSpc:PHA   :\ Scan for options, returns %ULIQxxxX, corrupts X
   9500\ AND #&80 BEQ skip JSR unlock
   9510JSR RomTest            :\ Y=&00 for ROM, 'R' for RAM, 'E' for EEPROM
   9520TYA:BEQ srPage1
   9530.errBankWritable
   9540JSR MkError:EQUB 138:EQUS "Bank is not ROM":BRK
   9550.srPage1
   9560LDX &F5:LDA #0
   9570STA ROMTABLE,X         :\ Remove from ROM table
   9580PLA:TAY                :\ Y='I' option
   9590PLA:CLC:ADC #&96:TAX   :\ X=sub-bank select value - confirmed &96 not 96
   9600PHP:SEI                :\ Stop IRQs while rebuilding ROM table
   9610OPT FNif(TARGET%>2)
   9620  LDA &FE34:PHA        :\ Get current 1MHz bus selection
   9630OPT FNendif
   9640TYA:PHA                :\ Save options
   9650JSR RomPage            :\ Select sub-bank via stack
   9660PLA                    :\ Get stacked options back
   9670\ AND #&40 BEQ skip JSR lock
   9680AND #&20               :\ Check 'I' option
   9690BNE RebuildROMs        :\ Rebuild ROM table
   9700BEQ AQRdone
   9730\ *AQRPAGE <page>
   9740\ ===============
   9750.AQRPAGE
   9760JSR ScanDec4:TAX     :\ Scan for a short hex number
   9770PHP:SEI              :\ Stop IRQs while rebuilding ROM table
   9780OPT FNif(TARGET%>2)
   9790  LDA &FE34:PHA      :\ Get current 1MHz bus selection
   9800  ORA #&20:STA &FE34 :\ Select cartridge bus
   9810OPT FNendif
   9820STX &FCFC            :\ Set paging register
   9830.RebuildROMs
   9840LDX #&0F             :\ Rebuild ROM table
   9850.AQRlp
   9860STX &F5:JSR L8D07    :\ Is it unplugged?
   9870.AQRunplugged
   9880LDA #0:BCS AQRstore  :\ Clear ROM table byte
   9890LDX &F5
   9900JSR ChkRomHeader     :\ Is there a ROM in the bank in &F5?
   9910BCS AQRunplugged     :\ No header, clear ROM table byte
   9920.AQRstore
   9930LDX &F5:STA ROMTABLE,X :\ Store in ROM table
   9940DEX:BPL AQRlp          :\ Step through all ROMs
   9950.AQRdone
   9960OPT FNif(TARGET%>2)
   9970  PLA:STA &FE34        :\ Restore 1MHz bus selection
   9980OPT FNendif
   9990PLP:RTS                :\ Restore IRQs and return
  10020\ *INSERT (*)(<rom>) (I)
  10030\ *UNPLUG (*)(<rom>) (U)
  10040\ ======================
  10050\ Updates:
  10060\ It is not an error to INSERT an inserted ROM.
  10070\ Only inserts into ROM table if has a ROM header and (I) option.
  10080\ Action is silent, no 'Press BREAK' message displayed.
  10090\
  10100\ On entry, A=0, (F2),Y=>parameters
  10110\
  10120.UNPLUG
  10130LDA #&FF                 :\ A=&FF - UNPLUG
  10140.INSERT
  10150PHA                      :\ Save <ins/unp>
  10160JSR L8CA0:PHP            :\ Scan for <rom>, save <rom>/<all> flag
  10170STA &F5                  :\ Save <rom>
  10180JSR srOptionsSpc:AND #&A0:\ Look for any options, %UxIxxxxx (corrupts X)
  10190PLP:BEQ L8D94            :\ No <rom> or *, insert/unplug all ROMs
  10200TAX:PLA:TAY:TXA:PHA      :\ Y=<ins/unp>, SP=>options
  10210LDX &F5:JSR InsertUnplugX:\ Insert or Unplug this ROM
  10220PLA                      :\ Get option %ULIQxxxx
  10230.InsertOption
  10240EOR #&20:BEQ InsertMe    :\ %xxIxxxxx - Insert
  10250EOR #&A0:BNE InsertDone  :\ %Uxxxxxxx - Unplug
  10260BEQ UnplugMe             :\ Remove me from ROM table
  10270.InsertMe
  10280JSR ChkRomHeader         :\ Check ROM header and get ROMTYPE
  10290.UnplugMe
  10300LDX &F5:STA ROMTABLE,X   :\ Insert or remove from ROM table
  10310.InsertDone
  10320RTS
  10330:
  10340\ Insert or unplug ROMs
  10350\ --------------------------------
  10360.L8D94
  10370\ On entry, CC=*    - silent
  10380\           CS=<cr> - prompt
  10390\           A=options
  10400\           SP=>ins/unp
  10410TAX:PLA:TAY:TXA:PHA      :\ X=options, Y=ins/unp, SP=>options
  10420LDX #&0F                 :\ Start at ROM 15
  10430.InsUnpLoop
  10440PHP:TYA:PHA              :\ Save silent flag, ins/unp
  10450BCC InsUnpSilent         :\ Skip prompt if *INSERT/UNPLUG *
  10460JSR L9030                :\ Print ROM info and prompt 'Y/N' (A,Y corrupted)
  10470BCS InsUnpSkip           :\ Skip if 'N'
  10480.InsUnpSilent
  10490PLA:TAY:PHA              :\ Y=Insert/Unplug
  10500STX &F5:JSR InsertUnplugX:\ Insert or Unplug this ROM, corrupts A,X,Y
  10510TSX:LDA &103,X           :\ A=options
  10520LDX &F5:JSR InsertOption :\ Deal with (I)(U) options
  10530.InsUnpSkip
  10540PLA:TAY:PLP              :\ Get ins/unp and silent flag back
  10550DEX:BPL InsUnpLoop       :\ Loop down from 15 to 0
  10560PLA                      :\ Drop options
  10570RTS
  10580:
  10590\ Insert or Unplug a ROM
  10600\ ----------------------
  10610\ A=ROM, Y=&FF, unplug ROM
  10620\ A=ROM, Y=&00, insert ROM
  10630\
  10640\ Update, do via OSBYTE 162
  10650\
  10660.InsertUnplug
  10670TAX
  10680.InsertUnplugX
  10690JSR XtoBitmap            :\ A=2^X, CC=0-7, CS=8-15
  10700INY:BNE InsertThisRom    :\ Y was &00, jump to insert
  10710BCC P%+3:INY
  10720ORA L0D6E,Y:STA L0D6E,Y  :\ Store into bitmap
  10730RTS
  10740.InsertThisRom
  10750EOR #&FF:BCS P%+3:DEY
  10760AND L0D6E,Y:STA L0D6E,Y  :\ Store into bitmap
  10770RTS
  10800\ *SRLOCK (<rom>)
  10810\ ===============
  10820.SRLOCK
  10830LDA #1              :\ A=1 to lock
  10840:
  10850\ *SRUNLOCK (<rom>)
  10860\ =================
  10870.SRUNLOCK           :\ Entered with A=0 to unlock
  10880PHA:JSR L8CA0       :\ Save lock/unlock flag, scan <rom> to A
  10890BNE P%+3:SEC        :\ No <rom> given, lock/unlock all ROMs
  10900TAX:PLA:TAY         :\ X=<rom>, Y=lock/unlock flag
  10910BCC LockUnlockROM   :\ <rom> given, lock/unlock that ROM
  10920                    :\ No <rom> given, drop into lock/unlocal all
  10930:
  10940\ Lock/Unlock all ROMs, Y=lock/unlock
  10950\ -----------------------------------
  10960.LockUnlockAll
  10970LDX #15
  10980.LockUnlockLp
  10990TXA:PHA:JSR LockUnlockROM
  11000PLA:TAX:DEX:BPL LockUnlockLp
  11010RTS
  11030\ Lock/Unlock ROM X, Y=0 unlock, Y=1 lock
  11040\ ---------------------------------------
  11050.LockUnlockROM
  11060TYA
  11070.LockUnlockROM1
  11080EOR LockAddrs,X      :\ Get lock address for this ROM
  11090BPL LockUnlockDone   :\ <&80=no lock register
  11100TAX                  :\ X=offset to lock register
  11110OPT FNif(TARGET%>2)
  11120  LDA &FE34:PHA      :\ Get current 1MHz bus selection
  11130  ORA #&20:STA &FE34 :\ Select cartridge bus
  11140OPT FNendif
  11150STA &FC00,X          :\ write to lock register
  11160OPT FNif(TARGET%>2)
  11170  PLA:STA &FE34      :\ Restore 1MHz bus selection
  11180OPT FNendif
  11190.LockUnlockDone
  11200RTS
  11210.LockAddrs
  11220EQUB &DC:EQUB &DE:EQUB &DC:EQUB &DE   :\ 0,1,2,3
  11230EQUB &00:EQUB &D8:EQUB &D8:EQUB &00   :\ 4,5,6,7
  11240EQUB &00:EQUB &00:EQUB &00:EQUB &00   :\ 8,9,10,11
  11250EQUB &00:EQUB &DA:EQUB &00:EQUB &DA   :\ 12,13,14,15
  11280\ Print character in A in inverted colours
  11290\ ----------------------------------------
  11300.L9002
  11310PHA
  11320LDA #&00:JSR L9018  :\ COLOUR 0
  11330LDA #&87:JSR L9018  :\ COLOUR 128+7
  11340PLA:JSR OSWRCH
  11350LDA #&07:JSR L9018  :\ COLOUR 7
  11360LDA #&80            :\ COLOUR 128+0
  11370.L9018
  11380PHA
  11390LDA #&11:JSR OSWRCH :\ VDU 17 - COLOUR
  11400PLA:JMP OSWRCH      :\ Colour number
  11420\ Display ROM info and Prompt Y/N
  11430\ -------------------------------
  11440\ On entry, X=ROM
  11450\ On exit, CS='N', CC='Y'
  11460\          A,Y corrupted
  11470\          X preserved
  11480.L9030
  11490TXA:PHA:JSR PrRomInfo :\ Print information on ROM X
  11500BCS L9062             :\ ROM is empty
  11510LDA #8:JSR OSWRCH
  11520PLA:TAX
  11530:
  11540\ Prompt Y/N
  11550\ ----------
  11560.L9035
  11570TXA:PHA
  11580JSR PrText:EQUS "? Y/N":EQUB 0
  11590.L903F
  11600JSR OSRDCH:BCS X9027  :\ Escape
  11610AND #&DF
  11620CMP #ASC"Y":BEQ L9056
  11630CMP #ASC"N":BNE L903F
  11640.L9056
  11650PHA:JSR PrText:EQUB 127:EQUB 127:EQUB 127:EQUB 0
  11660PLA:PHA
  11670JSR OSWRCH:JSR OSNEWL
  11680PLA:EOR #1:LSR A      :\ CS='N', CC='Y'
  11690.L9062
  11700PLA:TAX:RTS
  11710.X9027
  11720JMP errEscape         :\ Jump to Escape error
  11750\ Print ROM X information
  11760\ =======================
  11770\ Updates:
  11780\ Doesn't try to display information on empty ROMs
  11790\ Displays unplugged ROMs so *INSERT<cr> gets all ROMs
  11800\
  11810\ On exit, CS=nothing displayed, ROM empty, not RAM, not EEPROM
  11820\          CC=something displayed, occupied, or RAM, or EEPROM
  11830.PrRomInfo
  11840CLC
  11850.PrRomInfoAll
  11860PHP:STX &F5:JSR ChkRomHeader  :\ CLC=Ok, A=type; SEC=empty
  11870PHA:PHP                       :\ stack => romtype, header
  11880OPT FNif(TARGET%=0)
  11890  CMP #&60:BNE RomInfo0        :\ ROM is not BASIC
  11900  LDX &F5:CPX #10:BNE RomInfo0 :\ Not ROM 10 is not BASIC
  11910  PLP:BCC RomInfoDup:PHP       :\ ROM 10 is BASIC, duplicate of ROM 11
  11920  .RomInfo0
  11930OPT FNendif
  11940JSR RomTest               :\ Y=&00 for ROM, 'R' for RAM, 'E' for EEPROM
  11950PLP:BCC PrRomInfo1        :\ CLC, ROM header exists
  11960TYA:BNE PrRomInfo1        :\ Y<>0, SRAM or EEPROM
  11970.RomInfoDup
  11980PLA:TAX:PLP:BCS RomInfo2  :\ List all ROMs
  11990SEC:RTS                   :\ No ROM header, no SRAM/EEPROM
  12010\ stack=> romtype, Cy=ROM header, Y=SRAM
  12020.PrRomInfo1
  12030PLA:TAX                   :\ X=romtype, Cy=header, Y=sram
  12040PLA                       :\ Drop 'all ROMs' flag
  12050.RomInfo2
  12060PHP                       :\ stack => header
  12070TYA:PHA                   :\ stack => sram, header
  12080TXA:PHA                   :\ stack => romtype, sram, header
  12090:
  12100JSR PrText:EQUS "ROM ":EQUB 0
  12110LDX &F5:TXA:JSR PrNybble
  12120JSR PrSpace
  12130LDA L0D6D:AND #&8F
  12140CMP &F5:BNE RomInfo3      :\ Not default language
  12150LDA #ASC"*":JSR L9002     :\ Print inverted '*'
  12160BNE RomInfo4
  12170.RomInfo3
  12180LDA #ASC":":JSR OSWRCH
  12190.RomInfo4
  12200JSR PrText:EQUS " (":EQUB 0
  12210JSR L8D07:BCC RomInfo5      :\ Not unplugged
  12220PLA:LDA #ASC"U":JSR L9002   :\ Print inverted 'U'
  12230JSR PrSpace:BNE RomInfo6
  12240.RomInfo5
  12250LDY #ASC"S":PLA:PHA:JSR PrCharY
  12260LDY #ASC"L":PLA:ASL A:JSR PrCharY
  12270.RomInfo6
  12280PLA:BEQ RomInfo7            :\ ROM
  12290JSR L9002:BNE RomInfo8      :\ Print inverted 'R' or 'E'
  12300.RomInfo7
  12310JSR PrSpace
  12320.RomInfo8
  12330JSR PrText:EQUS ") ":EQUB 0
  12340PLP:BCS L917C               :\ No ROM header present
  12350:
  12360LDA #&08:STA &F6       :\ Point to before ROM title at &8009
  12370LDA #&80:STA &F7
  12380.L914A
  12390JSR RdRomNext          :\ Read next byte from ROM
  12400CMP #&00
  12410BNE P%+4:LDA #ASC" "   :\ Convert &00 to space
  12420CMP #ASC" ":BCC L914A  :\ Control code - skip
  12430CMP #ASC"(":BEQ L917C  :\ End display at (C) string
  12440.L915C
  12450LDY &0318:BEQ L917C    :\ X position=0, finish
  12460.L916D
  12470JSR OSWRCH             :\ Print character
  12480.L9170
  12490JMP L914A
  12500.L917C
  12510CLC:RTS                :\ CLC=occupied or empty+sram/eeprom
  12530.ChkRomHeader
  12540LDA #&05:STA &F6       :\ Point to before ROM type byte &8006
  12550JSR RdRom80:PHA        :\ Save ROM type byte
  12560JSR RdRomNext          :\ Get copyright offset from &8007
  12570STA &F6:JSR RdRom
  12580CMP #&00:BNE ChkRomHdrDone
  12590JSR RdRomNext
  12600CMP #ASC"(":BNE ChkRomHdrDone
  12610JSR RdRomNext
  12620CMP #ASC"C":BNE ChkRomHdrDone
  12630JSR RdRomNext
  12640CMP #ASC")":BNE ChkRomHdrDone
  12650PLA:CLC:RTS            :\ CLC=ok, A=ROM type
  12660.ChkRomHdrDone
  12670PLA:LDA #0             :\ Fake ROM type to be &00
  12680SEC:RTS                :\ SEC=no header
  12690.RdRom80
  12700LDA #&80:STA &F7
  12710.RdRomNext
  12720INC &F6
  12730.RdRom
  12740LDY &F5:JMP &FFB9
  12770\ ***************************
  12780\ ROM load/save/wipe commands
  12790\ ***************************
  12810\ *DELETEBGETROM <afsp> <rom>
  12820\ *RSAVE   <afsp> <rom>
  12830\ *SRSAVE  <afsp> <start> <end> <rom>
  12840\ *RLOAD   <afsp> <rom> (I) (L) (P) (Q) (R) (U)
  12850\ *DELETEACSROM <afsp> <rom> (I) (L) (P) (Q) (R) (U)
  12860\ *SRLOAD  <afsp> <start> <rom> (I) (L) (P) (Q) (R) (U)
  12870\ *ZERO    <rom>  (I) (L) (P) (Q) (R) (U)
  12880\ *SRWIPE  <rom>  (I) (L) (P) (Q) (R) (U)
  12890\ ===========================================
  12900\ <addr> <end> parameters not implemented
  12910\
  12920\ *SRWIPE <rom> (U) (L)
  12930\ =====================
  12940.SRWIPE
  12950LDA #&40:PHA          :\ Stack <wipe>
  12960JSR ScanDec4          :\ Scan <rom>
  12970CLC:BCC srWipeRom     :\ CLC=write zeros
  12990.srSyntax
  13000JSR MkError:EQUB 204:EQUS "Bad filename":BRK
  13010.errRomLocked
  13020JSR MkError:EQUB 135:EQUS "Bank not writable":BRK
  13040.SRLOAD
  13050LDA #&FF              :\ &FF=load
  13060.SRSAVE               :\ Enters with &00=save
  13070PHA:TYA:CLC           :\ Save load/save flag
  13080ADC &F2:STA FILEBLK+0 :\ Point to filename
  13090LDA #0
  13100ADC &F3:STA FILEBLK+1
  13110CLC:JSR GSINIT        :\ Use GSREAD to step past filename
  13120BEQ srSyntax          :\ No parameters
  13130.srskip
  13140JSR GSREAD:BCC srskip :\ Step past filename and trailing spaces
  13150.srgetnum
  13160TYA:PHA:DEY           :\ Save current parameter
  13170.srgetlp1
  13180INY
  13190.srgetlp2
  13200JSR CheckDigit:BCC srgetlp2 :\ Step past digits
  13210JSR SkipSpaces
  13220CMP #ASC"+":BEQ srgetlp1    :\ Step past '+' as well
  13230JSR CheckDigit:BCS srgetok  :\ Found the <rom>
  13240PLA:DEY:BCC srgetnum        :\ Loop to next param
  13250.srgetok
  13260PLA:TAY:JSR ScanDec4        :\ Get the <rom>
  13270.srWipeRom
  13280PHA:PHA             :\ Stack padding for <options>, stack <rom>
  13290:
  13300\ Now pointing to any trailing options or <cr>
  13310JSR srOptionsSpc    :\ Scan trailing options
  13320TSX:STA &102,X      :\ Save options on stack
  13330\
  13340\ Stack holds  <rom>, <options>, <&FF=load/&00=save/&40=wipe>
  13350\
  13360\ Check if <rom> is writable before we change MODE or attempt to load file
  13370ASL A               :\ Move (U)nlock option to Carry
  13380LDA &103,X          :\ Get action
  13390BEQ srPrecheck3     :\ &00=save, don't check if writable
  13400PLA:PHA:STA &F5     :\ Get <rom>, set &F5=<rom>
  13410EOR &F4             :\ Compare to myself (preserve Cy)
  13420BEQ errRomLocked    :\ Can't write to myself
  13430PHP:BCC srPrecheck1 :\ No (U)nlock option
  13440LDX &F5:LDY #0      :\ X=<rom>, Y=unlock
  13450JSR LockUnlockROM   :\ Unlock this ROM
  13460.srPrecheck1
  13470JSR RomTest         :\ Check ROM type in bank &F5
  13480DEY:BMI errRomLocked:\ Not SRAM or EEPROM
  13490PLP:BCC srPrecheck2 :\ Wasn't (U)nlocked, so don't relock
  13500LDX &F5:LDA #1      :\ X=<rom>, A=lock
  13510JSR LockUnlockROM1  :\ Relock this ROM
  13520.srPrecheck2
  13530TSX
  13540:
  13550LDA &103,X             :\ Get load/save/wipe action
  13560.srPrecheck3
  13570PHA:CMP #&40:BEQ P%+5  :\ Skip if *Wipe
  13580JSR CheckMode6         :\ Force to MODE 6
  13590:
  13600LDX #&80:LDY #&20      :\ Prepare for copy &8000->&2000
  13610PLA:SEC:BEQ srCopyData :\ Jump forward with &00=save
  13620CLC:BPL srWriteRom     :\ Jump forward with <&80=wipe
  13630JSR srFile:SEC         :\ Load <file>, SEC=Copy data
  13640\ Note, assume we lose &F5 through external call to OSFILE
  13650\
  13660.srWriteRom
  13670PLA:TAX:STX &F5        :\ X=ROM, also save in &F5
  13680LDA #0:STA ROMTABLE,X  :\ Remove from ROM table before overwriting
  13690TAY:PLA:PHA            :\ Y=0, check options
  13700BPL srWriteNoUnlock    :\ b7=0, don't unlock
  13710PHP:JSR LockUnlockROM:PLP :\ Unlock this ROM, preserving Carry
  13720.srWriteNoUnlock
  13730LDX #&20:LDY #&80      :\ Prepare for copy &2000->&8000
  13740LDA &F5:PHA            :\ Get ROM number back
  13750.srCopyData
  13760PLA:JSR RomCopy        :\ Copy data to/from selected <rom>
  13770\
  13780\ Stack now holds <options>, <load/save>
  13790\
  13800PLA:ASL A:PHA:BPL srNoLock :\ b6=0, don't lock
  13810LDX &F5:LDA #1
  13820JSR LockUnlockROM1         :\ Lock this ROM
  13830.srNoLock
  13840PLA:ASL A:BPL srNoInsert   :\ b5=0, don't insert
  13850JSR ChkRomHeader:BCS srNoInsert
  13860LDX &F5:STA ROMTABLE,X     :\ Store ROM type if valid header
  13870.srNoInsert
  13880PLA:BEQ srFile             :\ A=<load/save>, if &00, save copied ROM data
  13890.srDone
  13900RTS
  13910:
  13920.srFile
  13930PHA:LDX #15
  13940.srFileLp
  13950LDA srBlock,X:STA FILEBLK+2,X
  13960DEX:BPL srFileLp
  13970PLA:PHA:BMI srFileGo   :\ Jump to do Load
  13980LDA #&80:STA FILEBLK+3 :\ Load address=&FFFF8000
  13990LDA &2006              :\ Get ROM type byte
  14000ASL A:BPL srFileNoLang :\ No language
  14010SEC:LDY #2             :\ Prepare for no reloc address
  14020AND #&40:BEQ srsavelp2 :\ No relocation address, use &00008000
  14030LDX &2007              :\ Offset to (C)
  14040.srsavelp1
  14050INX:LDA &2000,X:BNE srsavelp1   :\ Step past (C) message
  14060CLC:LDY #0
  14070.srsavelp2
  14080BCS P%+5:LDA &2001,X            :\ Get Tube address
  14090STA FILEBLK+2,Y:STA FILEBLK+6,Y :\ Copy to control block
  14100INX:INY:TYA:AND #4:BEQ srsavelp2
  14110.srFileNoLang
  14120.srFileGo
  14130LDX #FILEBLK AND 255
  14140LDY #FILEBLK DIV 256
  14150PLA:JMP OSFILE
  14160.srBlock
  14170EQUD &FFFF2000
  14180EQUD &FFFF8000        :\ Low byte=&00 for load to address
  14190EQUD &FFFF2000
  14200EQUD &FFFF6000
  14210:
  14220\ Scan SRAM command options
  14230\ -------------------------
  14240\ (F2),Y=>any trailing options or <cr>
  14250\ On return, b7 downwards -> ULIQxxxX
  14260.srOptionsSpc
  14270JSR SkipSpaces
  14280.srOptions
  14290LDA #0:PHA            :\ Stack 'no options'
  14300LDA (&F2),Y           :\ Get first option
  14310.OptionLp1            :\ <cr> will fall through and set b0
  14320AND #&DF:LDX #7
  14330.OptionLp2
  14340CMP Options,X:BEQ OptionMatch
  14350DEX:BNE OptionLp2
  14360.OptionMatch
  14370PLA:ORA Bitmap,X:PHA
  14380JSR SkipSpaces1
  14390CMP #13:BNE OptionLp1
  14400PLA:RTS               :\ Return A=<options>
  14410.Options
  14420EQUS "....QILU"       :\ b0...b7, b0=no options at all
  14430:
  14440\ Select sub-bank in ROM in &F5, X=page select
  14450\ --------------------------------------------
  14460.RomPage
  14470LDA #RomPageEnd-RomPageStart            :\ Length of stacked code
  14480LDY #(RomPageEnd-RomCopyStart-1)AND255  :\ Offset to end of stacked code
  14490BNE StackCopySetupY                     :\ Jump to stack it and call it
  14500:
  14510\ Test if ROM in &F5 is SRAM
  14520\ --------------------------
  14530.RomTest
  14540LDA #RomTestEnd-RomTestStart            :\ Length of stacked code
  14550LDY #(RomTestEnd-RomCopyStart-1)AND255  :\ Offset to end of stacked code
  14560BNE StackCopySetup                      :\ Jump to stack it and call it
  14570:
  14580\ Copy between main RAM and SROM/SRAM
  14590\ -----------------------------------
  14600.RomCopy
  14610\ CC=zero data, CS=copy data
  14620\ X=source page, Y=dest page, A=rom
  14630STA &F5                 :\ ROM to copy to/from
  14640STX &A9:STY &AB         :\ Set source and dest
  14650LDA #&40:STA &AC        :\ Number of pages to copy
  14660LDA #RomCopyEnd-RomCopyStart           :\ Length of stacked code
  14670LDY #(RomCopyEnd-RomCopyStart-1)AND255 :\ Offset to end of stacked code
  14680.StackCopySetup
  14690LDX #0
  14700.StackCopySetupY
  14710STX &AD                      :\ Entry value for Y
  14720TSX:STX &AA                  :\ Save old stack pointer
  14730TAX                          :\ X=length of code to stack
  14740PHP:SEC:SBC #9:STA &A8:PLP   :\ Save offset to RomSelect code
  14750.StackCopyLp
  14760LDA RomCopyStart,Y:PHA       :\ Copy code to stack
  14770DEY:DEX:BNE StackCopyLp
  14780LDA &AA:PHA                  :\ Stack old stack pointer
  14790TSX                          :\ X=>entry address on stack
  14800PHP:TXA:CLC:ADC &A8:PLP      :\ Calculate address of RomSelect code
  14810STA &104,X                   :\ Adjust JSR destination
  14820PHP:SEI                      :\ Stop IRQs from calling while we fiddle with ROMs
  14830INX:JSR RomStackDispatch     :\ Call the code on the stack
  14840PLP:PLA:TAX:TXS              :\ Restore IRQs, restore stack
  14850RTS
  14860:
  14870\.RomCopyDispatch
  14880.RomStackDispatch
  14890LDA #&01:PHA            :\ Push stack address to jump to
  14900TXA:PHA
  14910LDY &AD:STY &A8:STY &AA :\ Set up entry values
  14920LDX &F5:LDA &F4
  14930RTS                     :\ Jump to code on stack
  14950\ Routine copied to stack to copy data
  14960\ ------------------------------------
  14970\ CC=zero data, CS=copy data
  14980\ A=old ROM, X=ROM to select, Y=0, (&A8)=src, (&AA)=dest, (&AC)=page count
  14990.RomStackStart
  15000.RomCopyStart
  15010PHA:JSR &100          :\ Select SRAM/SROM bank
  15020TYA                   :\ Prepare A=0 for wipe
  15030.RomCopyLp1
  15040LDX #&40              :\ Write 64 bytes at a time for EEPROM
  15050.RomCopyLp2
  15060BCC P%+4:LDA (&A8),Y  :\ CC=use zero, CS=fetch byte
  15070STA (&AA),Y           :\ Store byte
  15080INY:DEX:BNE RomCopyLp2
  15090DEY:PHP               :\ Save Carry
  15100.RomCopyCheck
  15110CMP (&AA),Y           :\ Check last byte written
  15120\ to do, this needs to explicitly check if bit 6 is toggling, not check for equality
  15130\ a locked EEPROM will toggle, but then return original data, resulting in a fixed loop
  15140\ Can return to caller Y=0 for Ok, or Y=63 for failed
  15150BNE RomCopyCheck      :\ Wait for data to settle
  15160PLP                   :\ Restore Carry
  15170INY:BNE RomCopyLp1    :\ Loop for 256 bytes
  15180INC &A9:INC &AB       :\ Increment addresses
  15190DEC &AC:BNE RomCopyLp1:\ Loop until all done
  15200PLA:TAX               :\ First 4 bytes and last 13 bytes
  15210.RomCopySelect        :\  are the same in both routines
  15220STX &F4
  15230LDA #&0C:STA ROMSEL
  15240STX ROMSEL:RTS        :\ 49 bytes
  15250.RomCopyEnd
  15270\ Routine copied to stack to test for sideways RAM
  15280\ ------------------------------------------------
  15290\ A=old ROM, X=ROM to select
  15300\ Returns Y=&00, 'R', 'E'
  15310.RomTestStart
  15320PHA:JSR &100                 :\ Select SRAM/SROM bank
  15330LDA &8008:EOR #&AA:STA &8008 :\ Modify version byte
  15340CMP &AAA2                    :\ Wait for floating bus to settle
  15350CMP &8008:PHP                :\ EQ=RAM, NE=ROM/EEPROM/empty
  15360EOR #&AA:STA &8008           :\ Restore byte
  15370.RomTestLp
  15380LDA &8008:EOR &8008:BEQ RomTestOk :\ Bytes match
  15390\ If empty socket, Cx EOR Cx -> 0x  %00xxxxxx
  15400\ If EEPROM      , Cx EOR 8x -> 4x  %x1xxxxxx
  15410ASL A:BPL RomTestOk          :\ Empty socket, bit 6 not toggling
  15420LDY #ASC"E":BNE RomTestLp    :\ Toggling, must be EEPROM/empty
  15430.RomTestOk
  15440PLP:BNE RomTestNotRam        :\ NE from earlier, ROM or EEPROM or empty
  15450LDY #ASC"R"                  :\ EQ from earlier, must be RAM
  15460.RomTestNotRam
  15470PLA:TAX
  15480.RomTestSelect
  15490STX &F4
  15500LDA #&0C:STA ROMSEL
  15510STX ROMSEL:RTS               :\ 52 bytes
  15520.RomTestEnd
  15540\ Routine copied to stack to select sub-banks
  15550\ -------------------------------------------
  15560\ A=old ROM, X=ROM to select, Y=paging value
  15570.RomPageStart
  15580PHA:JSR &100                 :\ Select SRAM/SROM bank
  15590STY &AFFF:STY &AFFF:STY &AFFF:\ Select sub-bank
  15600PLA:TAX
  15610.RomPageSelect
  15620STX &F4:LDA #12:STA ROMSEL
  15630STX ROMSEL:RTS               :\ 26 bytes
  15640.RomPageEnd
  15670\ *************
  15680\ Disk Commands
  15690\ *************
  15710\ *FORMAT <drv> <S|M|L> (Y)
  15720\ =========================
  15730.AFORMAT
  15740JSR L97E9:PHA           :\ Check ADFS, get drive number, A=0/1, X=0-F drive number
  15750JSR SkipSpaces:AND #&DF :\ Look for disk size
  15760CMP #ASC"S":BEQ Format2
  15770CMP #ASC"M":BEQ Format2
  15780CMP #ASC"L":BEQ Format2
  15790JSR MkError:EQUB 148:EQUS "Bad size":BRK
  15800:
  15810.Format2
  15820PHA:TXA:PHA            :\ stack => physical drive, size, logical drive
  15830JSR SkipSpaces1        :\ Look for final optional 'Y'
  15840AND #&DF:CMP #ASC"Y"
  15850PHP:BEQ Format3        :\ If 'Y', don't prompt for confirmation
  15860JSR PrText
  15870EQUS "Format drive ":EQUB 0
  15880TXA:JSR PrNybble
  15890JSR CheckEnabled
  15900:
  15910.Format3
  15920\ X=physical drive, stack => (Y), physical drive, size, logical drive
  15930JSR NMIClaim           :\ *FX143,12,255 - claim NMIs
  15940LDA #&00:STA FDCdrive  :\ Write &00 RESET to drive control register
  15950TAY:TAX
  15960.X9A96
  15970DEY:BNE X9A96          :\ Delay for while FDC resets
  15980DEX:BNE X9A96
  15990TSX:LDA &104,X         :\ Get drive number
  16000LDX #&00:JSR X93D1     :\ Write to drive register, noRES+SIDE0+DRIVE
  16010LDA #&07:JSR X93E0     :\ FDC command 'Restore', seek to Track 0
  16020PLP:BEQ Format11       :\ 'Y' option given, don't prompt to overwrite
  16030AND #&10:BNE Format11  :\ Sector Not Found - disk is unformatted
  16040JSR NMIRelease         :\ Release NMIs
  16050JSR PrText
  16060EQUS "Already formatted. Format":EQUB 0
  16070JSR CheckEnabled
  16080JSR NMIClaim           :\ *FX143,12,255 - claim NMIs
  16090LDA #&07:JSR X93E0     :\ Spin the disk up again
  16100:
  16110\ *FORMAT enabled, format existing disk enabled, NMI claimed, all ready to go
  16120\ stack => physical drive, size, logical drive
  16130.Format11
  16140PLA:STA L0935                :\ Store physical drive number
  16150LDX #80:LDY #1               :\ 80 tracks, 1+1 sides
  16160PLA:CMP #ASC"L":BEQ Format12 :\ Large =80x2
  16170DEY:CMP #ASC"M":BEQ Format12 :\ Medium=80x1
  16180LDX #40                      :\ Small =40x1
  16190.Format12
  16200STX L0932:STY L0931          :\ &932=tracks, &931=sides-1
  16210PLA:STA L0930                :\ &930=logical drive number 0/1
  16220LDA #&00:STA L0938           :\ Set to side 0
  16230:
  16240\ Now moved to FILEBLK
  16250\ &930=logical drive number
  16260\ &931=sides-1
  16270\ &932=tracks
  16280\ &933=old NMI owner
  16290\ &934=
  16300\ &935=physical drive number
  16310\ &936=physical drive number as ASCII for *MOUNT, etc
  16320\ &937=
  16330\ &938=current side
  16340\ &939=bottom of memory low byte
  16350\ &93A=bottom of memory high byte
  16360\ &93B=current track
  16370\ &93C=logical sector number
  16380\ &93D=
  16390\ &93E=modified track number, track+8 if odd track
  16400\ &93F=physical sector number
  16410:
  16420JSR CheckMode
  16430JSR L97B4:STX L0939          :\ OSBYTE &83 - read top of user memory
  16440TYA:SEC:SBC #&1A:STA L093A   :\ Use 6.5K of memory below screen
  16450:
  16460.Format13
  16470JSR PrText
  16480EQUS "Formatting ":EQUB 0
  16490JSR PrPreTrack               :\ Print drive,track
  16500:
  16510.L9338
  16520LDA FDCstatus         :\ Read FDC status
  16530ROR A:BCS L9338       :\ Loop until FDC not BUSY
  16540\
  16550\ Note, FDCstatus in A register is now rotated right
  16560\ So, must test for status DIV 2
  16570\
  16580AND #&20:BEQ L935C    :\ Not WriteProtected
  16590OPT FNif(TARGET%<0)
  16600  BNE L935C           :\ Ignore WriteProtected
  16610OPT FNendif
  16620JSR NMIRelease
  16630JSR MkError:EQUB 201:EQUS "Disk read only":BRK
  16640.FormatEscape
  16650JSR NMIRelease
  16660JMP errEscape
  16680\ FDC ready, writable, and disk spinning
  16690\ --------------------------------------
  16700.L935C
  16710LDA #&00:STA L093B    :\ current track=0
  16720.L9360
  16730BIT &FF:BMI FormatEscape  :\ Escape pressed
  16740JSR X9567             :\ Print track number, build image, write to disk
  16750INC L093B:LDA L093B   :\ Increment track
  16760CMP L0932:BNE L9389   :\ Compare with number of tracks, jump if not done
  16770DEC L0931:BMI L939A   :\ Decrement number of sides, jump if all done
  16780LDA #&00:JSR X93E0    :\ Clear FDC command register
  16790INC L0938             :\ Increment side number
  16800JSR X93D0             :\ Write to drive control register, select side 1
  16810JMP L9338             :\ Jump back to format from track 0
  16830\ Prepare to do next track
  16840\ ------------------------
  16850.L9389
  16860LDA FDCstatus         :\ Read FDC status
  16870ROR A:BCS L9389       :\ Loop until not BUSY
  16880LDA #&53              :\ &53=StepToNext, u=update, h=no spin, v=no verify, speed=3
  16890JSR X93E0             :\ Step to next track
  16900JMP L9360             :\ Jump back to write next track
  16920\ All done
  16930\ --------
  16940.L939A
  16950JSR OSNEWL
  16960.L93A8
  16970LDA FDCstatus         :\ Read FDC status
  16980ROR A:BCS L93A8       :\ Loop until not BUSY
  16990LDA #&00:JSR X93E0    :\ Clear FDC command register
  17000JSR NMIRelease        :\ Release NMIs
  17010:
  17020\ Write padding file if early ADFS version
  17030\ ----------------------------------------
  17040LDA L0936:ORA #ASC"4":PHA :\ Save ASCII floppy drive number for later
  17050LDA #&08:STA &F6      :\ Point to version number
  17060LDA #&80:STA &F7
  17070LDY &0DBC:JSR &FFB9   :\ Read from current filing system ROM
  17080CMP #0:BNE X9A34      :\ Not &00, no need for padding file
  17090:
  17100JSR PrText
  17110EQUS "Writing ADFS 1.00 ZYSysHelp":EQUB 0
  17120]:cmdLen=1:cmdBuf=&B00:IF(opt%AND2):cmdLen=X9A59-X9A39:cmdBuf=&B00-cmdLen
  17130[OPT opt%
  17140LDX #cmdLen-1
  17150.X9A0F
  17160LDA X9A39,X:STA cmdBuf,X :\ Copy commands to top of page A
  17170DEX:BPL X9A0F
  17180PLA:PHA                  :\ Get ASCII drive number
  17190STA cmdBuf+&05           :\ Store in '*DIR'
  17200STA cmdBuf+&0D           :\ Store in '*DELETEBGET'
  17210STA cmdBuf+&28           :\ Store in '*ACCESS'
  17220LDX #(cmdBuf AND 255)+0
  17230JSR X9980                :\ Do '*DIR'
  17240LDX #(cmdBuf AND 255)+7
  17250JSR X9980                :\ Do '*DELETEBGET'
  17260LDX #(cmdBuf AND 255)+&20
  17270JSR X9980                :\ Do '*ACCESS'
  17280JSR OSNEWL
  17290.X9A34
  17300PLA                      :\ Mount the drive and finish
  17310\ Could finish by verifying the drive
  17320:
  17330\ Mount drive in A
  17340\ ----------------
  17350.X9975
  17360TAY:LDX #7
  17370.X9978
  17380LDA X9A59,X:STA &00A8,X    :\ Copy command to &A8-&AF
  17390DEX:BPL X9978
  17400STY &AE                    :\ Store ASCII drive number
  17410LDX #&A8:LDY #0            :\ Do '*MOUNT'
  17420LDA #3:JMP (FSCV)          :\ Pass '*MOUNT' to filing system
  17430.X9980
  17440LDY #&0A:JMP OS_CLI        :\ Do command in page &A
  17460.X9A39
  17470EQUS "DIR :x":EQUB 13
  17480EQUS "SAVE :x.ZYSysHelp 0+38F0":EQUB 13
  17490EQUS "ACCESS :x.ZYSysHelp L":EQUB 13
  17500.X9A59
  17510EQUS "MOUNT x":EQUB 13
  17520.X9A69
  17540\ Create image of track data in memory, then write to disk
  17550\ --------------------------------------------------------
  17560.X9567
  17570LDA L0939:STA LAE+0    :\ LAE/F=>bottom of data buffer
  17580LDA L093A:STA LAE+1
  17590LDA #&08
  17600JSR OSWRCH:JSR OSWRCH  :\ Print two backspaces
  17610LDA L0938:LSR A        :\ Side
  17620LDA L093B              :\ Track
  17630BCC P%+4:ADC #79       :\ Add 80 for side 1
  17640JSR PrHex              :\ Print track number
  17650JSR L9433              :\ Create track image in memory
  17660.X9585
  17670LDA FDCstatus          :\ Read FDC status
  17680ROR A:BCS X9585        :\ Loop until FDC not busy
  17690LDA L0939:STA LAC+0    :\ LAC/1=>bottom of memory
  17700LDA L093A:STA LAC+1
  17710:
  17720\ Write track data
  17730\ ----------------
  17740.X99A7
  17750OPT FNif(TARGET%>-1)
  17760  SEI:LDY #&00
  17770  .X99AE
  17780  LDA FDCstatus:AND #&21  :\ Read FDC Spin+Busy status
  17790  CMP #&20:BNE X99AE      :\ Not spun up, loop until spinning
  17800  LDA #&F4:STA FDCcommand :\ Write FDC command &F4=Write track, h=spin, e=no delay
  17810  LDY #&9
  17820  .X99EF
  17830  DEY:BNE X99EF           :\ Delay
  17840  .X99C6
  17850  LDA (LAC),Y             :\ Get byte from track data
  17860  CMP #&FF:BEQ X99EC      :\ &FF=end of data
  17870  TAX                     :\ X=data to write
  17880  .X99CD
  17890  LDA FDCstatus
  17900  AND #&02:BEQ X99CD      :\ Loop until DataRequest
  17910  STX FDCdata             :\ Write data
  17920  INY:BNE X99C6           :\ Loop for 256 bytes
  17930  INC LAC+1:BNE X99C6     :\ Loop for next 256 bytes
  17940  .X99EC
  17950  CLI
  17960OPT FNendif
  17970:
  17980.X959A
  17990LDA FDCstatus          :\ Read FDC status
  18000ROR A:BCS X959A        :\ Loop until FDC not busy
  18010ROL A:RTS              :\ Return result in A
  18030\ Write command A to FDC, return result in A
  18040\ ------------------------------------------
  18050.X93E0
  18060SEI:STA FDCcommand  :\ Write to FDC command register
  18070LDY #&09
  18080.X93EB
  18090DEY:BNE X93EB       :\ Delay for 9 loops
  18100CLI:BEQ X959A
  18120\ Select drive and side
  18130\ ---------------------
  18140.X93D0
  18150LDA L0930             :\ Get current drive
  18160LDX L0938             :\ Get current side
  18170.X93D1
  18180BEQ P%+4:ORA #fdcSIDE :\ If side 1, OR with SIDE
  18190CLC:ADC #1            :\ Convert to drive select bitmap
  18200ORA #fdcRES           :\ OR with noRESET
  18210STA FDCdrive:RTS
  18230\ Create a track in memory
  18240\ ------------------------
  18250.L9433
  18260LDA #&4E:LDX #&3C:JSR X95AF  :\ Fill &3C x &4E byte
  18270LDX #&00                     :\ Start at physical sector 0
  18280.L943C
  18290STX L093F:JSR L9459          :\ Create sector X
  18300LDX L093F:INX                :\ Increment sector
  18310CPX #&10:BNE L943C           :\ Loop for 16 sectors
  18320LDA #&FF                     :\ Write terminating &FF byte
  18340\ Fill memory with track data
  18350\ ---------------------------
  18360.X95AD
  18370LDX #&01            :\ Fill one byte of memory
  18380.X95AF
  18390LDY #&00
  18400STX L093E           :\ Number of bytes to store
  18410LDX #&00
  18420.X95B6
  18430STA (LAE),Y:INC LAE+0
  18440BNE X95BE:INC LAE+1
  18450.X95BE
  18460INX:CPX L093E:BNE X95B6
  18470RTS
  18490\ Fill a sector with fill byte
  18500\ ----------------------------
  18510JSR L9452
  18520.L9452
  18530LDA #&4E                     :\ Fill byte &4E
  18540.L9454
  18550LDX #&00:BEQ X95AF           :\ Fill with 256 bytes
  18570\ Create a single sector image
  18580\ ----------------------------
  18590.L9459
  18600LDA #&00:LDX #&0C:JSR X95AF  :\ 12 bytes of &00
  18610LDA #&F5:LDX #&03:JSR X95AF  :\  3 bytes of &F5
  18620LDA #&FE:JSR X95AD           :\  1 bytes of &FE - ID start flag
  18630LDA L093B:JSR X95AD          :\ current track
  18640LDA L0938:JSR X95AD          :\ current side
  18650LDA L093B:ROR A:PHP          :\ current track
  18660ROL A:PLP:BCC L9483:ADC #&07 :\ track+8 for odd tracks
  18670.L9483
  18680STA L093E
  18690CLC:LDA L093F:ADC L093E      :\ Physical sector+track offset
  18700AND #&0F:STA L093C           :\ Logical sector number
  18710JSR X95AD                    :\ logical sector
  18720LDA #&01:JSR X95AD           :\ &01 = sector size=256
  18730LDA #&F7:JSR X95AD           :\ &F7 - tell FDC to generate a CRC
  18740LDA #&4E:LDX #&16:JSR X95AF  :\ 22 bytes of &4E
  18750LDA #&00:LDX #&0C:JSR X95AF  :\ 12 bytes of &00
  18760LDA #&F5:LDX #&03:JSR X95AF  :\  3 bytes of &F5
  18770LDA #&FB:JSR X95AD           :\  1 byte of &FB - data start flag
  18780:
  18790LDA L0938:ORA L093B          :\ Check track and side
  18800BEQ L94C4                    :\ If track 0, side 0, fill with root data
  18810.L94C1
  18820JMP L9556                    :\ Jump to fill with empty sector
  18840\ Fill sectors on track 0 with root data
  18850\ --------------------------------------
  18860.L94C4
  18870LDA L093C                    :\ Logical sector number
  18880CMP #&07:BCS L94C1           :\ End of track 0, normal blank sector
  18890PHA:LDA #&00:JSR L9454       :\ Fill sector with &00
