  18900DEC LAE+1                    :\ Step back to start of sector
  18910PLA     :BEQ L950F           :\ Sector 0 - FSM starts
  18920CMP #&02:BCC L9523           :\ Sector 1 - FSM lengths
  18930         BEQ L9547           :\ Sector 2 - start of '$'
  18940CMP #&06:BCS X94E7           :\ Sector 6 - end of '$'
  18950         JMP X9732           :\ Sector 3-5 - middle of '$'
  18970\ Sector 0 - Free Space Map starts
  18980\ --------------------------------
  18990.L950F
  19000LDY #&00
  19010LDA #&07:STA (LAE),Y         :\ Free space starts at &000007
  19020JSR X9712                    :\ Get disk size in sectors to &XXAA
  19030LDY #&FC:STA (LAE),Y         :\ Store total number of sectors
  19040TXA:INY:STA (LAE),Y
  19050BNE X9727                    :\ Jump forward to calculate checksum
  19070\ Sector 1 - Free Space Map lengths
  19080\ ---------------------------------
  19090.L9523
  19100JSR X9712:LDY #0             :\ Get disk size in sectors to &XXAA
  19110SEC:SBC #&07:STA (LAE),Y     :\ Free space length = size-7
  19120TXA:SBC #&00
  19130INY:STA (LAE),Y:LDY #&FB
  19140LDA &240                     :\ Get random-ish value from countdown timer
  19150STA (LAE),Y:INY              :\ &FB=DiskID
  19160LDA &295                     :\ Get random-ish value from TIME
  19170STA (LAE),Y                  :\ &FC=DiskID
  19180LDA #&03:INY:INY:STA (LAE),Y :\ End of FSM list
  19190BNE X9727                    :\ Jump forward to calculate checksum
  19210\ Sector 2 - start of '$' directory
  19220\ ---------------------------------
  19230.L9547
  19240LDY #0
  19250.X9549
  19260LDA Hugo,Y:INY:STA (LAE),Y   :\ Copy 'Hugo' to start of directory
  19270CPY #&04:BNE X9549
  19280BEQ X9732
  19300\ Sector 6 - end of '$' directory
  19310\ -------------------------------
  19320.X94E7
  19330LDA #ASC"$"
  19340LDY #&CC:STA (LAE),Y  :\ Directory name
  19350LDY #&D9:STA (LAE),Y  :\ Directory title
  19360LDA #&0D
  19370INY:STA (LAE),Y       :\ Terminate with <cr>
  19380LDY #&CD:STA (LAE),Y  :\ Terminate with <cr>
  19390LDY #&D6
  19400LDA #&02:STA (LAE),Y  :\ Parent directory
  19410LDY #&FB
  19420.X9502
  19430LDA Hugo-&FB,Y:STA (LAE),Y :\ Copy 'Hugo' to end of directory
  19440INY:CPY #&FF:BNE X9502
  19450BEQ X9732
  19470\ Calculate FSM checksum
  19480\ ----------------------
  19490.X9727
  19500LDA #&FF:LDY #&FE:CLC
  19510.X9999
  19520ADC (LAE),Y:DEY:BNE X9999    :\ Add up 254 down to 1
  19530ADC (LAE),Y                  :\ Add byte 0
  19540DEY:STA (LAE),Y              :\ Store at offset 255
  19550:
  19560.X9732
  19570INC LAE+1                    :\ Point back to end of sector data
  19580BNE L955B                    :\ Jump to add sector footer
  19600.L9556
  19610LDA #&6C:JSR L9454           :\ 256 bytes of &6C
  19620.L955B
  19630LDA #&F7:JSR X95AD           :\ &F7 - tell FDC to generate a CRC
  19640LDA #&4E:LDX #&2C:JMP X95AF  :\ 44 bytes of &4E
  19660\ Work out disk size in sectors
  19670\ -----------------------------
  19680.X9712
  19690LDX #&0A
  19700LDA L0931:BNE X970D :\ If two sides, return X=&0A, A=&00
  19710LDX #&05
  19720LDA L0932           :\ Tracks
  19730CMP #80:BEQ X970D   :\ If 80 tracks, return X=&05, A=&00
  19740LDX #&02:LDA #&80   :\ Otherwise, 40 tracks, return X=&02, A=&80
  19750RTS
  19760.X970D
  19770LDA #0:RTS
  19790.CheckMode
  19800JSR L97A6            :\ Read current screen mode
  19810INY:INY:BNE ChkModeChk
  19820.CheckMode6
  19830JSR L97A6            :\ Read current screen mode
  19840.ChkModeChk
  19850CPY #6:BCS ChkModeOk :\ MODE>6 or MODE>4, don't change MODE
  19860LDA #22:JSR OSWRCH   :\ Need more memory, change to MODE 7 (drops to MODE 6)
  19870LDA #7:JMP OSWRCH
  19880.ChkModeOk
  19890RTS
  19910.PrVerifyTrack
  19920JSR PrText
  19930EQUS "Verifying ":EQUB 0
  19940.PrPreTrack
  19950LDA L0935:JSR PrNybble
  19960STA L0936           :\ Store ASCII drive number
  19970LDA #ASC":":JSR OSWRCH
  19980LDA #0:JMP PrHex
  20010\ *VERIFY <drv>
  20020\ =============
  20030.VERIFY
  20040JSR L97E9:PHA         :\ Check ADFS, get drive number
  20050JSR X9975             :\ *MOUNT the drive
  20060LDA #0:STA L093B      :\ Start with track 0
  20070STA L09D8:STA L09D7   :\ Sector=0
  20080PLA:STA L0935         :\ Store logical drive number
  20090ASL A:ASL A:ASL A     :\ drive*32
  20100ASL A:ASL A:STA L09D6 :\ Sector=drive*32+000000
  20110LDA #&10:STA L09EF    :\ 16 sectors per call
  20120JSR CheckMode
  20130JSR L97B4:STX LAE+0          :\ OSBYTE &83 - read top of user memory
  20140TYA:SEC:SBC #&12:STA LAE+1   :\ Use 5K of memory below screen
  20150:
  20160\ FBLK+0  &93B=current track
  20170\         OSWORD &72 control block
  20180\ FBLK+1  &9D0=result
  20190\ FBLK+2  &9D1=    \
  20200\ FBLK+3  &9D2=     \ address
  20210\ FBLK+4  &9D3=&FF  /
  20220\ FBLK+5  &9D4=&FF /
  20230\ FBLK+6  &9D5=command
  20240\ FBLK+7  &9D6=drive*32+sector
  20250\ FBLK+8  &9D7=sector
  20260\ FBLK+9  &9D8=sector
  20270\ FBLK+10 &9D9=count
  20280\ FBLK+11 &9DA=0
  20290\ FBLK+12 &9EF=sectors to do per call
  20300\ FBLK+13 &9EC\
  20310\ FBLK+14 &9ED } disk size
  20320\ FBLK+15 &9EE/
  20330\ FBLK+16 &935=physical drive number
  20340\ FBLK+17 &936=ASCII drive number for *MOUNT
  20360\ Verify loop
  20370\ -----------
  20380.Verify10
  20390LDA #&86:JSR OSBYTE
  20400TXA:BNE Verify11
  20410JSR PrVerifyTrack      :\ Print 'Verifying...'
  20420.Verify11
  20430BIT &FF:BMI VerifyEscape
  20440LDA #&08
  20450JSR OSWRCH:JSR OSWRCH  :\ Print two backspaces
  20460LDA L093B:JSR PrHex    :\ Print track number
  20470:
  20480LDX #0:STX L09D0       :\ Result
  20490STX L09DA              :\ Byte 10
  20500LDA LAE+0:STA L09D1
  20510LDA LAE+1:STA L09D2
  20520DEX
  20530STX L09D3:STX L09D4    :\ Address high word
  20540LDA #8:STA L09D5       :\ Command
  20550LDA L09EF:STA L09D9    :\ Count
  20560LDX #L09D0 AND 255     :\ Point to control block
  20570LDY #L09D0 DIV 256
  20580LDA #&72:JSR OSWORD    :\ Read sectors
  20590LDX L09D0:BNE Verify12 :\ Report disk error
  20600:
  20610LDA L093B:BNE Verify13 :\ Not track 0, step to next track
  20620LDY #&FC               :\ Point to disk size
  20630.X9664
  20640LDA (LAE),Y            :\ Get disk size
  20650STA L09EC-&FC,Y        :\ Copy disk size to &9EC/D/E
  20660INY:CPY #&FF:BNE X9664
  20670:
  20680OPT FNif(TARGET%<0)
  20690  LDA #&0A:STA L09ED
  20700  LDA #0:STA L09EC:STA L09EE
  20710OPT FNendif
  20720BEQ Verify13           :\ Step to next track
  20730.VerifyEscape
  20740JMP errEscape
  20760.Verify12
  20770JSR PrText             :\ Report disk error
  20780EQUS " disc error ":EQUB 0
  20790TXA:JSR PrHex:JSR OSNEWL
  20800:
  20810.Verify13
  20820CLC:LDA L09EF          :\ Get sectors/track
  20830ADC L09D8:STA L09D8    :\ Update sector number low
  20840BCC Verify14:INC L09D7 :\ Update sector number mid
  20850BNE Verify14:INC L09D6 :\ Update sector number high
  20860.Verify14
  20870SEC
  20880LDA L09EC:SBC L09EF:STA L09EC  :\ Decrement number to do
  20890LDA L09ED:SBC #0:STA L09ED
  20900LDA L09EE:SBC #0:STA L09EE
  20910ORA L09ED:BNE Verify15         :\ >256 to do, loop back
  20920LDA L09EC:BEQ VerifyDone       :\ Nothing left
  20930CMP #&10:BCS Verify15          :\ >15 to do, loop back
  20940STA L09EF                      :\ <16 to do, do remaining amount
  20950.Verify15
  20960INC L093B                      :\ Increment track number
  20970JMP Verify10                   :\ Loop back for next track
  20980.VerifyDone
  20990LDA L0936:JSR X9975 :\ *MOUNT the drive
  21000JMP OSNEWL          :\ Done
  21020.NMIClaim
  21030LDX #&0C:LDY #&FF
  21040JSR X97BB           :\ *FX143,12,255 - claim NMIs
  21050LDA #&40:STA &D00   :\ Null NMI routine
  21060STY L0933:RTS       :\ Save old NMI owner
  21080.NMIRelease
  21090LDX #&0B:LDY L0933    :\ *FX143,11,<num> - release NMIs
  21100.X97BB
  21110LDA #&8F:BNE L97B8    :\ Service call
  21120.L97A6
  21130LDA #&87:BNE L97B8    :\ Read screen mode
  21140.L97B2
  21150LDA #&7C:BNE L97B8    :\ Acknowledge Escape
  21160.L97B4
  21170LDA #&84              :\ Top of user memory
  21180.L97B8
  21190JMP OSBYTE
  21220\ Check FS and parse drive number if ADFS
  21230\ =======================================
  21240.L97E9
  21250TYA:PHA
  21260LDA #0:TAY:JSR OSARGS
  21270CMP #8:BEQ L97EC  :\ Filing system is ADFS
  21280OPT FNif(TARGET%<0)
  21290  BNE L97EC       :\ Ignore current filing system
  21300OPT FNendif
  21310PLA               :\ Drop Y
  21320PLA:PLA           :\ Drop return address to command
  21330PLA:PLA           :\ Drop return address to command dispatcher
  21340LDY &F5:LDX &F4   :\ Restore to return to MOS
  21350LDA #4
  21360.L97EB
  21370RTS
  21380.L97EC
  21390PLA:TAY           :\ Get command pointer back
  21400                  :\ Check for valid drive number
  21410                  :\ 0,1,4,5,A,B,E,F
  21420                  :\ Should also allow leading <colon>
  21430LDA (&F2),Y       :\ Get drive character
  21440JSR CheckDigit    :\ Check if valid character, INY if valid
  21450BCS errBadDrive
  21460TAX:CMP #8        :\ Keep original drive number in X
  21470BCC P%+4:SBC #2   :\ Convert to logical drive number
  21480AND #3:CMP #2:BCC L97EB
  21490.errBadDrive
  21500JSR MkError:EQUB 205:EQUS "Bad drive":BRK
  21510:
  21520.CheckEnabled
  21530JSR L9035:BCC L97EB     :\ Prompt Y/N?
  21540JSR MkError:EQUB 146:EQUS "Aborted":BRK
  21550:
  21560.Hugo
  21570EQUS "Hugo"
  21580:
  21590.RelocTable
  21600]NEXT:PROCsm_table
  21610OSCLI "*Save "+file$+" "+STR$~(mcode%+mclen%)+" "+STR$~O%+" FFFF0000 FFFBBC00"
  21620OSCLI "Stamp "+file$
  21630END
  21640:
  21650DEFFNsm_pass(pass%)
  21660IFpass%=0:mclen%=0
  21670IFpass%=1:mclen%=O%-mcode%
  21680P%=&8100-128*(pass%AND2)
  21690O%=mcode%+mclen%*(pass%AND2)DIV2
  21700IFpass%=1:IF O%+mclen%*2.125>L%:PRINT"Code overrun":END
  21710=VALMID$("4646",pass%+1,1)
  21720:
  21730DEFPROCsm_table
  21740base80%=mcode%+mclen%:base81%=mcode%:byte%=0:count%=0:off%=0:REPEAT
  21750byte80%=base80%?off%:byte81%=base81%?off%:IFoff%>=mclen%:byte80%=&80:byte81%=&80
  21760IF((byte81%-byte80%) AND &FE)<>0:PRINT "ERROR: Offset by more than one page at &";~&8000+off%
  21770IF(byte80% AND &C0)=&80:byte%=byte%DIV2+128*(byte81%-byte80%):count%=count%+1
  21780IFcount%=8:?O%=byte%:O%=O%+1:byte%=0:count%=0
  21790off%=off%+1:UNTILoff%>=mclen% AND count%=0
  21800ENDPROC
#+end_src

* eof.
